> AGENT

Both already coexist in the current image — the trick is `CLAUDE_INSTALL_DIR`.

`Dockerfile.base:121-127` installs the `claude` binary to `/usr/local/bin/claude` (outside `$HOME`), and `Dockerfile.base:148` sets `CLAUDE_INSTALL_DIR=/usr/local/bin` in `/etc/environment`. That env var tells `claude --update` to write the new binary to `/usr/local/bin/claude` instead of `~/.local/bin/claude`. `entrypoint.sh:16` then chowns the binary to the sandbox user so updates work without sudo (commit d07852e).

So even when `/home/$USER` is bind-mounted from the host, `claude` lives entirely under `/usr/local/bin` and never touches `~/.local`. The host's `~/.local` (which on macOS would be the wrong arch anyway) is simply ignored.

If you ever wanted Claude to live under `~`, the alternatives would be: (a) mount a container-local volume on top of `~/.local` to mask the host's copy, or (b) have the entrypoint symlink `~/.local/bin/claude` → `/usr/local/bin/claude`. But the env-var override is the cleanest and is what's in place today.

> DEVELOPER

how can we no mount the $HOME and still not have to reauth claude and codex for every new sandbox?

> AGENT

The auth state for both tools lives in tiny dotdirs:
- Claude Code: `~/.claude/` (mainly `.credentials.json` + `settings.json`)
- Codex: `~/.codex/` (mainly `auth.json`)

You don't need to persist all of `$HOME` to keep the login — just those two directories. A few options, cleanest first:

**1. Per-user mini-volumes (recommended)** — keep `$HOME` ephemeral (or container-local), and bind-mount just two small per-user paths:
```
/data/users/{name}/auth/claude  →  /home/{name}/.claude
/data/users/{name}/auth/codex   →  /home/{name}/.codex
```
Add them to the mount list in `SandboxManager` alongside the existing home/data mounts. Survives sandbox destroy/recreate; nothing else leaks between sessions.

**2. Inject at entrypoint** — same idea but copy the credential files in from `/data/users/{name}/auth/` during `entrypoint.sh` (next to where it injects SSH keys at line 23). Works if you'd rather not have a live bind-mount, but you also need to copy back on logout/refresh, which is fragile because tokens can rotate mid-session.

**3. Shared OAuth proxy** — have one host-level login per user and expose it via env vars or a socket. Overkill for this; both CLIs are designed around the dotdir model.

I'd go with #1: smallest change, matches how SSH keys and the data volume already work, and the auth dirs are small enough that BTRFS subvolumes per user are cheap. Want me to sketch the `SandboxManager` change?

```
222	  def start(sandbox:)
223	    raise Error, "Sandbox is destroyed" if sandbox.status == "destroyed"
224	    return sandbox if sandbox.status == "running"
225	
226	    user = sandbox.user
227	
228	    # Try to restart the existing container to preserve Sysbox UID mapping.
229	    # Recreating the container assigns a new UID offset, which causes
230	    # bind-mounted files to appear owned by the wrong user.
231	    if sandbox.container_id.present?
232	      restarted = try_restart_existing(sandbox)
233	      if restarted
234	        TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.tailscale? && user.tailscale_enabled?
235	        RouteManager.new.reconnect_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?
236	        return sandbox
237	      end
238	    end
239	
240	    # Container gone or restart failed — recreate from scratch.
241	    ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)
242	
243	    # Refresh image info in case the image was updated since sandbox creation
244	    info = fetch_image_info(sandbox.image)
245	    sandbox.update!(image_id: info[:image_id], image_built_at: info[:image_built_at], image_version: info[:image_version])

247	    create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)
248

251	
252	    sandbox

254	    sandbox.update!(status: "destroyed", container_id: nil)

257	
258	  def rebuild(sandbox:)
259	    raise Error, "Sandbox is destroyed" if sandbox.status == "destroyed"
260	
261	    user = sandbox.user
262	
263	    # Destroy the existing container so we get a fresh one with the latest image.
264	    if sandbox.container_id.present?
265	      begin
266	        old = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
267	        old.stop(t: 5) rescue nil
268	        old.delete(force: true)
269	      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
270	        # already gone
271	      end
272	    end
273	
274	    ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)
275	
276	    info = fetch_image_info(sandbox.image)
277	    sandbox.update!(image_id: info[:image_id], image_built_at: info[:image_built_at], image_version: info[:image_version])
278	
279	    create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)
280	
281	    TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.tailscale? && user.tailscale_enabled?
282	    RouteManager.new.reconnect_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?
283	
284	    sandbox
285	  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
286	    sandbox.update!(status: "destroyed", container_id: nil)
287	    raise Error, "Container not found — sandbox must be recreated"
288	  end
289	
290	  def stop(sandbox:)
291	    return sandbox if sandbox.status == "stopped"
292	
293	    begin
294	      TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
295	    rescue TerminalManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
296	      # best-effort terminal cleanup
297	    end
298	
299	    begin
300	      VncManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
301	    rescue VncManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
302	      # best-effort VNC cleanup
303	    end
304	
305	    RouteManager.new.suspend_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?
306	
307	    if sandbox.container_id.present?
308	      begin
309	        container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
310	        container.stop(t: 10)
311	      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
312	        # already gone
313	      end
314	    end
315	
316	    sandbox.update!(status: "stopped")
317	    sandbox
318	  end
319	
320	  SERVICES = %w[docker vnc].freeze
321	
322	  def service_start(sandbox:, service:)
323	    raise Error, "Unknown service: #{service}" unless SERVICES.include?(service)
324	    raise Error, "Sandbox has no container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
325	
326	    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
327	    user = sandbox.user.name
328	
329	    case service
330	    when "docker"
331	      container.exec([ "bash", "-c", "pgrep -x dockerd > /dev/null && echo already_running && exit 0; dockerd --storage-driver=overlay2 --mtu=$(ip link show eth0 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'mtu \\K[0-9]+' || echo 1500) &>/var/log/dockerd.log & echo started" ])
332	    when "vnc"
333	      geometry = sandbox.vnc_geometry || "1280x900"
334	      depth = sandbox.vnc_depth || 24
335	      container.exec([ "bash", "-c", "pgrep -x Xvnc > /dev/null && echo already_running && exit 0; su -s /bin/bash #{user} -c 'Xvnc :99 -rfbport 5900 -SecurityTypes None -AlwaysShared -geometry #{geometry} -depth #{depth} &>/var/log/xvnc.log &' && su -s /bin/bash #{user} -c 'DISPLAY=:99 openbox &>/var/log/openbox.log &' && websockify -addr :6080 -target localhost:5900 -url /websockify &>/var/log/websockify.log & echo started" ])
336	    end
337	  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
338	    raise Error, "Container not found"
339	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
340	    raise Error, "Failed to start #{service}: #{e.message}"
341	  end
342	
343	  def service_stop(sandbox:, service:)
344	    raise Error, "Unknown service: #{service}" unless SERVICES.include?(service)
345	    raise Error, "Sandbox has no container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
346	
347	    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
348	
349	    case service
350	    when "docker"
351	      container.exec([ "bash", "-c", "pkill -x dockerd 2>/dev/null; pkill -x containerd 2>/dev/null; echo stopped" ])
352	    when "vnc"
353	      container.exec([ "bash", "-c", "pkill -x websockify 2>/dev/null; pkill -x openbox 2>/dev/null; pkill -x Xvnc 2>/dev/null; echo stopped" ])
354	    end
355	  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
356	    raise Error, "Container not found"
357	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
358	    raise Error, "Failed to stop #{service}: #{e.message}"
359	  end
360	
361	  def logs(sandbox:, tail: 200, timestamps: false)
362	    raise Error, "Sandbox has no container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
363	
364	    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
365	    container.logs(stdout: true, stderr: true, follow: false, tail: tail, timestamps: timestamps)
366	      .force_encoding("UTF-8").encode("UTF-8", invalid: :replace, undef: :replace, replace: "")
367	  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
368	    raise Error, "Container not found"
369	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
370	    raise Error, "Failed to fetch logs: #{e.message}"
371	  end
372	
373	  def status(sandbox:)
374	    return { state: "destroyed" } if sandbox.container_id.blank?
375	
376	    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
377	    info = container.json
378	    {
379	      state: info.dig("State", "Status"),
380	      running: info.dig("State", "Running"),
381	      started_at: info.dig("State", "StartedAt"),
382	      pid: info.dig("State", "Pid")
383	    }
384	  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
385	    { state: "not_found" }
386	  end
387	
388	  # Create a composite snapshot (Docker image + optional BTRFS layers).
389	  # Returns a Snapshot ActiveRecord object.
390	  #
391	  # layers: array of "container", "home", "data"
392	  #   nil means "all available" based on sandbox config
393	  def create_snapshot(sandbox:, name:, label: nil, layers: nil, data_subdir: nil)
394	    raise Error, "Sandbox has no running container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
395	
396	    user = sandbox.user
397	    name = name.presence || Date.today.iso8601
398	    requested_layers = layers&.map(&:to_s) || %w[container home data]
399	
400	    snap = Snapshot.new(
401	      user: user,
402	      name: name,
403	      label: label,
404	      source_sandbox: sandbox.name,
405	      data_subdir: data_subdir
406	    )
407	
408	    # ── Container layer ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
409	    if requested_layers.include?("container")
410	      repo = "sc-snap-#{user.name}"
411	      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
412	      image = container.commit(
413	        repo: repo,
414	        tag: name,
415	        comment: "sandbox:#{sandbox.name}"
416	      )
417	      snap.docker_image = "#{repo}:#{name}"
418	      snap.docker_size  = image.info["Size"]
419	    end
420	
421	    # ── Home layer (BTRFS only) ───────────────────────────────────────────────
422	    if requested_layers.include?("home") && sandbox.mount_home? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
423	      home_src  = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/home"
424	      home_dest = "#{DATA_DIR}/snapshots/#{user.name}/#{name}/home"
425	      if Dir.exist?(home_src)
426	        BtrfsHelper.snapshot_subvolume(home_src, home_dest)
427	        snap.home_snapshot = home_dest
428	        snap.home_size     = BtrfsHelper.subvolume_size(home_dest)
429	      end
430	    end
431	
432	    # ── Data layer (BTRFS only) ───────────────────────────────────────────────
433	    if requested_layers.include?("data") && sandbox.data_path.present? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
434	      if data_subdir.present?
435	        data_src  = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}/#{data_subdir}".chomp("/")
436	      else
437	        data_src  = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
438	      end
439	      data_dest = "#{DATA_DIR}/snapshots/#{user.name}/#{name}/data"
440	      if Dir.exist?(data_src)
441	        BtrfsHelper.snapshot_subvolume(data_src, data_dest)
442	        snap.data_snapshot = data_dest
443	        snap.data_size     = BtrfsHelper.subvolume_size(data_dest)
444	      end
445	    end
446	
447	    snap.save!
448	    snap
449	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
450	    raise Error, "Failed to create snapshot: #{e.message}"
451	  rescue BtrfsHelper::Error => e
452	    raise Error, "Failed to snapshot filesystem layer: #{e.message}"
453	  end
454	
455	  # Legacy alias kept for backward compatibility (used by existing API endpoint).
456	  def snapshot(sandbox:, name: nil, **opts)
457	    snap = create_snapshot(sandbox: sandbox, name: name, layers: %w[container])
458	    {
459	      name: snap.name,
460	      image: snap.docker_image,
461	      sandbox: snap.source_sandbox,
462	      created_at: snap.created_at
463	    }
464	  end
465	
466	  def list_snapshots(user:)
467	    import_legacy_snapshots(user)
468	    Snapshot.where(user: user).order(created_at: :desc).map { |s| snapshot_json(s) }
469	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
470	    raise Error, "Failed to list snapshots: #{e.message}"
471	  end
472	
473	  def find_snapshot(user:, name:)
474	    import_legacy_snapshots(user)
475	    Snapshot.find_by!(user: user, name: name)
476	  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
477	    raise Error, "Snapshot '#{name}' not found"
478	  end
479	
480	  def destroy_snapshot(user:, name:)
481	    snap = Snapshot.find_by(user: user, name: name)
482	
483	    if snap
484	      # Remove Docker image
485	      if snap.docker_image.present?
486	        begin
487	          Docker::Image.get(snap.docker_image).remove
488	        rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
489	          # Already gone
490	        rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
491	          raise Error, "Failed to remove Docker image: #{e.message}"
492	        end
493	      end
494	
495	      # Remove BTRFS snapshots
496	      if snap.home_snapshot.present?
497	        begin
498	          BtrfsHelper.delete_snapshot(snap.home_snapshot)
499	        rescue BtrfsHelper::Error => e
500	          Rails.logger.warn("Could not delete home snapshot #{snap.home_snapshot}: #{e.message}")
501	        end
502	      end
503	
504	      if snap.data_snapshot.present?
505	        begin
506	          BtrfsHelper.delete_snapshot(snap.data_snapshot)
507	        rescue BtrfsHelper::Error => e
508	          Rails.logger.warn("Could not delete data snapshot #{snap.data_snapshot}: #{e.message}")
509	        end
510	      end
511	
512	      snap.destroy!
513	    else
514	      # Legacy: try to find and remove Docker image directly
515	      image_ref = "sc-snap-#{user.name}:#{name}"
516	      begin
517	        Docker::Image.get(image_ref).remove
518	      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
519	        raise Error, "Snapshot '#{name}' not found"
520	      rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
521	        raise Error, "Failed to destroy snapshot: #{e.message}"
522	      end
523	    end
524	  end
525	
526	  def restore(sandbox:, snapshot_name:, layers: nil)
527	    user = sandbox.user
528	    was_tailscale = sandbox.tailscale?
529	
530	    snap = Snapshot.find_by(user: user, name: snapshot_name)
531	    requested_layers = layers&.map(&:to_s)
532	
533	    # Determine the Docker image to use
534	    if snap&.docker_image.present?
535	      image_ref = snap.docker_image
536	    else
537	      # Fall back to legacy naming
538	      image_ref = "sc-snap-#{user.name}:#{snapshot_name}"
539	    end
540	
541	    restore_container = requested_layers.nil? || requested_layers.include?("container")
542	    restore_home      = snap&.home_snapshot.present? && (requested_layers.nil? || requested_layers.include?("home"))
543	    restore_data      = snap&.data_snapshot.present? && (requested_layers.nil? || requested_layers.include?("data"))
544	
545	    # Validate the Docker image exists if we need it
546	    if restore_container
547	      begin
548	        Docker::Image.get(image_ref)
549	      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
550	        raise Error, "Snapshot '#{snapshot_name}' not found"
551	      end
552	    end
553	
554	    begin
555	      TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
556	    rescue TerminalManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
557	      # best-effort terminal cleanup
558	    end
559	
560	    begin
561	      VncManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
562	    rescue VncManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
563	      # best-effort VNC cleanup
564	    end
565	
566	    if sandbox.tailscale?
567	      TailscaleManager.new.disconnect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox)
568	    end
569	
570	    if sandbox.container_id.present?
571	      begin
572	        old_container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
573	        old_container.stop(t: 5) rescue nil
574	        old_container.delete(force: true)
575	      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
576	        # Already gone
577	      end
578	    end
579	
580	    # ── Restore home directory ────────────────────────────────────────────────
581	    if restore_home
582	      home_target = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/home"
583	      begin
584	        BtrfsHelper.restore_subvolume(snap.home_snapshot, home_target)
585	      rescue BtrfsHelper::Error => e
586	        Rails.logger.warn("Could not restore home snapshot: #{e.message}")
587	      end
588	    end
589	
590	    # ── Restore data directory ────────────────────────────────────────────────
591	    if restore_data
592	      if snap.data_subdir.present?
593	        data_target = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}/#{snap.data_subdir}".chomp("/")
594	      else
595	        data_target = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
596	      end
597	      begin
598	        BtrfsHelper.restore_subvolume(snap.data_snapshot, data_target)
599	      rescue BtrfsHelper::Error => e
600	        Rails.logger.warn("Could not restore data snapshot: #{e.message}")
601	      end
602	    end
603	
604	    # ── Recreate container ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
605	    final_image = restore_container ? image_ref : sandbox.image
606	    # Clear stale container reference before recreating — prevents the record
607	    # from pointing at a dead container if create_container_and_start fails.
608	    sandbox.update!(image: final_image, container_id: nil, status: "pending")
609	
610	    create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)
611	
612	    if was_tailscale && user.tailscale_enabled?
613	      TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox)
614	    end
615	
616	    sandbox
617	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
618	    raise Error, "Failed to restore snapshot: #{e.message}"
619	  end
620	
621	  # Import legacy Docker-only snapshots as DB records (idempotent).
622	  def import_legacy_snapshots(user)
623	    repo_prefix = "sc-snap-#{user.name}"
624	
625	    Docker::Image.all.each do |img|
626	      repo_tags = img.info["RepoTags"] || []
627	      repo_tags.each do |tag|
628	        repo, tag_name = tag.split(":")
629	        next unless repo == repo_prefix
630	        next if tag_name.blank?
631	        next if Snapshot.exists?(user: user, name: tag_name)
632	
633	        Snapshot.create!(
634	          user: user,
635	          name: tag_name,
636	          docker_image: tag,
637	          docker_size: img.info["Size"],
638	          source_sandbox: img.info.dig("Comment")&.delete_prefix("sandbox:"),
639	          created_at: Time.at(img.info["Created"] || Time.current.to_i)
640	        )
641	      end
642	    end
643	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError, ActiveRecord::RecordInvalid => e
644	    Rails.logger.warn("Legacy snapshot import failed: #{e.message}")
645	  end
646	
647	  def snapshot_json(snap)
648	    {
649	      name: snap.name,
650	      label: snap.label,
651	      source_sandbox: snap.source_sandbox,
652	      layers: snap.layers,
653	      docker_image: snap.docker_image,
654	      docker_size: snap.docker_size,
655	      home_size: snap.home_size,
656	      data_size: snap.data_size,
657	      total_size: snap.total_size,
658	      created_at: snap.created_at
659	    }
660	  end
661	
662	  def connect_info(sandbox:)
663	    user = sandbox.user.name
664	
665	    unless sandbox.tailscale?
666	      raise Error, "SSH access requires Tailscale. Enable Tailscale on your account and connect this sandbox to your tailnet."
667	    end
668	
669	    ts_ip = wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
670	    raise Error, "Tailscale IP not available — is the Tailscale sidecar running?" unless ts_ip.present?
671	
672	    {
673	      host: ts_ip,
674	      port: 22,
675	      user: user,
676	      command: "ssh #{user}@#{ts_ip}",
677	      tailscale_ip: ts_ip
678	    }
679	  end
680	
681	  # Update the SMB password on all active SMB-enabled sandboxes for a user.
682	  def update_smb_password(user:)
683	    raise Error, "No SMB password set" unless user.smb_password.present?
684	
685	    user.sandboxes.active.where(smb_enabled: true).find_each do |sandbox|
686	      next if sandbox.container_id.blank?
687	
688	      begin
689	        container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
690	        set_smb_password(container, user)
691	      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
692	        # Container gone, skip
693	      rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
694	        Rails.logger.warn("Failed to update SMB password for #{sandbox.full_name}: #{e.message}")
695	      end
696	    end
697	  end
698	
699	  private
700	
701	  def wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox:, max_attempts: 30, delay: 0.5)
702	    # Wait for the sandbox to be provisioned (background job) and Tailscale IP to be assigned
703	    max_attempts.times do
704	      sandbox.reload # Refresh from DB to get latest status
705	
706	      # If sandbox isn't running yet, keep waiting (provision job in progress)
707	      if sandbox.status != "running"
708	        sleep delay
709	        next
710	      end
711	
712	      # Sandbox is running, try to get Tailscale IP
713	      ts_ip = TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
714	      return ts_ip if ts_ip.present?
715	
716	      sleep delay
717	    end
718	    Rails.logger.warn("Tailscale IP not available for sandbox #{sandbox.id} after #{max_attempts} attempts (status: #{sandbox.status})")
719	    nil
720	  end
721	
722	  # Try to start the existing container in-place (preserves Sysbox UID mapping).
723	  # Returns true if the container was successfully restarted, false if it should
724	  # be recreated (container gone, or start failed).
725	  def try_restart_existing(sandbox)
726	    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
727	    container.start
728	    container.refresh!
729	    unless container.json.dig("State", "Running")
730	      container.delete(force: true) rescue nil
731	      return false
732	    end
733	    sandbox.update!(status: "running")
734	    true
735	  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
736	    false
737	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
738	    Rails.logger.warn("SandboxManager: restart existing container failed: #{e.message}, will recreate")
739	    begin
740	      container&.stop(t: 5) rescue nil
741	      container&.delete(force: true)
742	    rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
743	      # best-effort cleanup
744	    end
745	    false
746	  end
747	
748	  # Extracts the image SHA and BUILD_DATE from a local Docker image.
749	  # Returns a hash with :image_id and :image_built_at (or nils on error).
750	  def fetch_image_info(image_ref)
751	    img    = Docker::Image.get(image_ref)
752	    config = img.json.dig("Config") || {}
753	    labels = config["Labels"] || {}
754	
755	    date_str = labels["org.opencontainers.image.created"] || labels["BUILD_DATE"]
756	    if date_str.nil?
757	      env_entry = (config["Env"] || []).find { |e| e.start_with?("BUILD_DATE=") }
758	      date_str  = env_entry&.split("=", 2)&.last
759	    end
760	
761	    {
762	      image_id:       img.id,
763	      image_built_at: date_str ? Time.zone.parse(date_str) : nil,
764	      image_version:  labels["org.opencontainers.image.version"]
765	    }
766	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError, ArgumentError, TypeError
767	    { image_id: nil, image_built_at: nil, image_version: nil }
768	  end
769	
770	  def connect_to_network(container)
771	    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
772	    network.connect(container.id)
773	  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
774	    Rails.logger.warn("SandboxManager: network #{NETWORK_NAME} not found, skipping network connection")
775	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
776	    Rails.logger.warn("SandboxManager: failed to connect container to #{NETWORK_NAME}: #{e.message}")
777	  end
778	
779	  def container_env(user, sandbox)
780	    env = [
781	      "SANDCASTLE_USER=#{user.name}",
782	      "SANDCASTLE_SSH_KEY=#{user.all_ssh_keys_text}"
783	    ]
784	    env << "USER_EMAIL=#{user.email_address}" if user.email_address.present?
785	    env << "USER_FULLNAME=#{user.full_name}" if user.full_name.present?
786	    env << "GITHUB_USERNAME=#{user.github_username}" if user.github_username.present?
787	    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED=#{sandbox.vnc_enabled? ? '1' : '0'}"
788	    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY=#{sandbox.vnc_geometry}"
789	    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH=#{sandbox.vnc_depth}"
790	    env << "SANDCASTLE_DOCKER_ENABLED=#{sandbox.docker_enabled? ? '1' : '0'}"
791	    env << "SANDCASTLE_SMB_ENABLED=#{sandbox.smb_enabled? ? '1' : '0'}"
792	    env << "SANDCASTLE_HOME_PERSISTED=#{sandbox.mount_home ? '1' : '0'}"
793	    env << "SANDCASTLE_DATA_PERSISTED=#{sandbox.data_path.present? ? '1' : '0'}"
794	    env << "SANDCASTLE_DATA_PATH=#{sandbox.data_path}" if sandbox.data_path.present?
795	    env
796	  end
797	
798	  def container_runtime
799	    @container_runtime ||= begin
800	      runtimes = Docker.info["Runtimes"] || {}
801	      if runtimes.key?("sysbox-runc")
802	        "sysbox-runc"
803	      else
804	        Rails.logger.warn("SandboxManager: sysbox-runc not available, falling back to runc (Docker-in-Docker will not work inside sandboxes)")
805	        "runc"
806	      end
807	    end
808	  end
809	
810	  # mkdir_p with self-healing: if EACCES, fix the parent dir's ownership
811	  # via a short-lived Docker container (only way when running as non-root
812	  # inside a container with Docker socket access).
813	  def ensure_dir(path)
814	    FileUtils.mkdir_p(path)
815	  rescue Errno::EACCES
816	    docker_chown(File.dirname(path))
817	    FileUtils.mkdir_p(path)
818	  end
819	
820	  # Ensure a bind-mounted directory is world-writable so the sandbox user
821	  # (non-root inside the Sysbox container) can write to it.
822	  def prepare_bind_mount(path)
823	    stat = File.stat(path)
824	    return if stat.mode & 0o777 == 0o777
825	    return if system("/usr/bin/sudo", "-n", "/usr/bin/chmod", "777", path)
826	    docker_chmod(path, "777")
827	  rescue Errno::ENOENT
828	    # directory disappeared — race condition, ignore
829	  end
830	
831	  # Fix ownership and permissions of a host path via sudo (preferred) or a busybox container fallback.
832	  def docker_chown(path)
833	    return if system("/usr/bin/sudo", "-n", "/usr/bin/chown", "#{Process.uid}:#{Process.gid}", path) &&
834	             system("/usr/bin/sudo", "-n", "/usr/bin/chmod", "755", path)
835	    docker_run_fix(path, "sh", "-c", "chown #{Process.uid}:#{Process.gid} /mnt && chmod 755 /mnt")
836	  end
837	
838	  def docker_chmod(path, mode)
839	    docker_run_fix(path, "chmod", mode, "/mnt")
840	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
841	    Rails.logger.warn("docker_chmod(#{path}) failed: #{e.message}")
842	  end
843	
844	  def docker_run_fix(host_path, *cmd)
845	    image = fix_image
846	    c = Docker::Container.create(
847	      "Image" => image, "Cmd" => cmd,
848	      "HostConfig" => { "Binds" => [ "#{host_path}:/mnt" ] }
849	    )
850	    c.start
851	    result = c.wait(30)
852	    exit_code = result&.dig("StatusCode") || -1
853	    unless exit_code == 0
854	      raise Error, "docker_run_fix failed (exit #{exit_code}) for #{host_path}: #{cmd.join(' ')}"
855	    end
856	  ensure
857	    c&.delete(force: true) rescue nil
858	  end
859	
860	  # Pick an image guaranteed to be on this Docker daemon.
861	  # Prefer busybox (tiny), fall back to alpine, then any local image.
862	  def fix_image
863	    %w[busybox:latest alpine:latest].each do |img|
864	      begin
865	        return img if Docker::Image.get(img)
866	      rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
867	        next
868	      end
869	    end
870	    # Last resort: use any image already present
871	    all = Docker::Image.all
872	    raise Error, "No local images available for docker_run_fix" if all.empty?
873	    tags = all.first.info["RepoTags"]
874	    tags&.first || all.first.id
875	  end
876	
877	  # Inject SMB password into the container via exec, avoiding env var leakage.
878	  def set_smb_password(container, user)
879	    return unless user.smb_password.present?
880	
881	    username = user.name
882	    password = user.smb_password
883	    container.exec(
884	      ["bash", "-c", "printf '%s\\n%s\\n' \"$0\" \"$0\" | smbpasswd -a -s \"$1\" 2>&1 || echo 'Warning: smbpasswd failed' >&2",
885	       password, username]
886	    )
887	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
888	    Rails.logger.warn("set_smb_password failed for #{user.name}: #{e.message}")
889	  end
890	
891	  def volume_binds(user, sandbox)
892	    binds = []
893	    if sandbox.mount_home
894	      binds << "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/home:/home/#{user.name}"
895	    end
896	    if sandbox.data_path.present?
897	      host_path = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
898	      binds << "#{host_path}:/persisted"
899	    end
900	    # Chrome profile persistence: mount separately if not mounting full home
901	    if user.chrome_persist_profile? && !sandbox.mount_home
902	      host_path = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/chrome-profile"
903	      binds << "#{host_path}:/home/#{user.name}/.config/google-chrome"
904	    end
905	    binds
906	  end
907	end
908
```

**1. Constant + a single user toggle.** In `app/services/sandbox_manager.rb`:

```ruby
# Per-user persisted dotdirs that get bind-mounted even when the full $HOME isn't.
# Lets Claude/Codex (and similar tools) keep their auth across fresh sandboxes.
AUTH_PATHS = {
  ".claude" => "auth/claude",   # Claude Code: .credentials.json, settings.json
  ".codex"  => "auth/codex"     # Codex: auth.json
}.freeze
```

**2. Extend `ensure_mount_dirs`** (after the existing chrome-profile block at line 138-142):

```ruby
    dir = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/#{rel}"
    ensure_dir(dir)
    prepare_bind_mount(dir)
  end

```

**3. Extend `volume_binds`** (after the chrome-profile block at line 901-904):

```ruby

  AUTH_PATHS.each do |container_rel, host_rel|
    host_path = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/#{host_rel}"
```

Plus the obvious touches: permit the param in `SettingsController`, add a checkbox to `settings/show.html.erb`, and BTRFS subvolumes (`BtrfsHelper.create_user_data_subvolume`-style) for `auth/claude` and `auth/codex` if you want cheap snapshots.

**Caveats worth flagging before you wire it up:**

- **First-login bootstrap.** Empty `~/.claude` means the first sandbox still has to do the OAuth dance — but it'll write `.credentials.json` into the bind-mounted dir, so every *subsequent* sandbox is logged in. If you want zero-touch from sandbox #1, you'd need a one-shot login flow on the web side that drops the credential file into `/data/users/{name}/auth/claude/` directly.
- **Sysbox UID remapping.** Same trick used today for chrome-profile (`prepare_bind_mount` chmods 777). Should just work.
- **Shared state across sandboxes.** All of a user's sandboxes share one `~/.claude` — concurrent `claude` sessions writing to `~/.claude/projects/` could race, but for credentials/settings it's fine.
- **Generalizing later.** If this list grows past 3–4 entries, lift `AUTH_PATHS` to a per-user JSON column (`persisted_paths`) so users can add `.config/gh`, `.npmrc`, etc. themselves without a migration. For now, hardcoded is simpler.

> AGENT

Got it — flipping the design. Let Claude live where it wants (`~/.local/bin/claude`), keep `$HOME` ephemeral (per-container writable layer), and bind-mount only the *settings/credential* dirs from a per-user persistent area.

## The flipped plan

**Mount topology per sandbox** (no `$HOME` mount):
```
/home/{user}                       ← container-local (ephemeral, in writable layer)
  ├── .local/bin/claude            ← container-local; updates via `claude --update`
  ├── .claude/                     ← bind-mounted from /data/users/{user}/settings/claude
  ├── .codex/                      ← bind-mounted from /data/users/{user}/settings/codex
  ├── .gitconfig                   ← container-local (entrypoint regenerates from env)
  └── .ssh/authorized_keys         ← container-local (entrypoint injects)
```

The principle: **binaries and OS state are container-local; user identity/credentials/settings are per-user persistent dirs surgically mounted in.**

### Image changes (`images/sandbox/Dockerfile.base`)

1. Move the claude install from `/usr/local/bin/claude` to `/etc/skel/.local/bin/claude` so `useradd -m` copies it into each new sandbox user's home automatically:
   ```dockerfile
   RUN install -d -m 755 /etc/skel/.local/bin \
       && curl -fsSL "...$VERSION/$PLATFORM/claude" -o /etc/skel/.local/bin/claude \
       && chmod +x /etc/skel/.local/bin/claude
   ```
2. Drop the `CLAUDE_INSTALL_DIR=/usr/local/bin` line — claude defaults to `~/.local/bin`, which is now the right answer.
3. Verify `~/.local/bin` is on PATH for non-login shells (Ubuntu's `~/.profile` only adds it for login shells; SSH non-interactive doesn't source it). Add `/etc/profile.d/local-bin.sh` with `PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"`.

### Entrypoint changes (`images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh`)

- Drop the `chown "$USERNAME" /usr/local/bin/claude` line (no longer needed — the binary is in `~/.local/bin/claude` already owned by the user via skel copy).
- Useradd already happens fresh on every new container, so skel-copy fires automatically. Existing recursive chown of `/home/$USER` covers it.

### `SandboxManager` changes

Same as the previous sketch, just renamed to reflect intent — these are *settings*, not just *auth*:

```ruby
SETTINGS_PATHS = {
  ".claude" => "settings/claude",
  ".codex"  => "settings/codex"
}.freeze
```

Always-on (no user toggle needed — this is the new default behavior). Bind-mount block in `volume_binds` and dir-creation block in `ensure_mount_dirs` exactly as before, but unconditionally instead of gated on `mount_home`.

### What this gets you

- **Claude installs and updates wherever it pleases** (`~/.local/bin`) — no `CLAUDE_INSTALL_DIR` hack.
- **Each sandbox starts with the image's pinned claude version**, not whatever stale binary lives in a shared home — that's a feature, not a bug. Image rebuilds = version bumps for everyone.
- **Auth survives sandbox destroy/recreate** because `~/.claude/.credentials.json` lives on the host.
- **Per-sandbox `claude --update`** is allowed but ephemeral (lost on container recreate). If a user wants the new version persistently, they bump the image.
- **No `mount_home` flag for the common case.** Keep `mount_home` as an opt-in escape hatch for users who really want a shared `$HOME`.

### Things to decide before implementing

1. **Codex auth file** — confirm where Codex actually stores the token (`~/.codex/auth.json` is my guess; check on a current sandbox before committing the path).
2. **`~/.config/claude` vs `~/.claude`** — newer Claude versions sometimes use XDG paths. Worth a `find ~ -name 'credentials*'` after a real login to be sure we're mounting the right dir.
3. **Backwards compat** — existing sandboxes with `mount_home: true` should keep working unchanged. The new behavior only kicks in for `mount_home: false`.
4. **First-login UX** — still requires one OAuth dance per user (in their first sandbox). Acceptable, or do you want a host-side "log in once" web flow that pre-populates `/data/users/{name}/settings/claude/`?

> AGENT

Injection is the right model for *static* credentials but has one real footgun for *OAuth-style* ones. Let me lay out both the mechanism and the gotcha.

## Why injection works (mostly)

The SSH key case is simple because the credential is **read-only inside the sandbox** — sshd consumes `authorized_keys`, never rewrites it. Inject once, done.

Claude Code and Codex are different:
- **API key auth** (`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `OPENAI_API_KEY`): static, never written back. Injection is perfect — could be plain env vars, no file needed.
- **OAuth login** (Claude Pro/Max, Codex sign-in): the CLI refreshes the access token periodically and rewrites `~/.claude/.credentials.json` (and Codex's `auth.json`). With injection + no write-back, every new sandbox starts with a stale token and forces a refresh — usually fine, but **if Anthropic rotates the refresh token on use, sandbox A's copy gets invalidated when sandbox B refreshes.** Race condition with real consequences.

So: injection is great for API keys and the *initial* OAuth credential. For OAuth long-term, you either need (a) write-back, or (b) a bind-mount on just `~/.claude` and `~/.codex` (the previous sketch).

## Two injection mechanisms (both already exist as patterns in the codebase)

**Pattern A — env var, like SSH keys** (`entrypoint.sh:22-34`): simple, but env vars are visible in `docker inspect` and survive in container metadata. Fine for SSH pubkeys (already public). Risky for OAuth tokens.

**Pattern B — `docker exec` after start, like SMB password** (`sandbox_manager.rb:877-889`): set after container is up, never appears in env or `docker inspect`. The right channel for secrets.

## Generic "files to inject" sketch — Pattern B

A small new model:

```ruby
# db/migrate/..._create_injected_files.rb
create_table :injected_files do |t|
  t.references :user, null: false, foreign_key: true
  t.string :path, null: false        # relative to $HOME, e.g. ".claude/.credentials.json"
  t.text :content                    # encrypted via `encrypts :content`
  t.integer :mode, default: 0o600, null: false
  t.timestamps
end
add_index :injected_files, [:user_id, :path], unique: true
```

```ruby
# app/models/injected_file.rb
class InjectedFile < ApplicationRecord
  belongs_to :user
  encrypts :content
  validates :path, presence: true, format: { without: %r{\.\./} }  # no path traversal
end
```

In `SandboxManager` (mirroring `set_smb_password` at line 877):

```ruby
def inject_files(container, user)
  user.injected_files.find_each do |f|
    container.exec([
      "bash", "-c",
      <<~SH,
        set -e
        target="/home/$1/$2"
        mkdir -p "$(dirname "$target")"
        printf '%s' "$3" > "$target"
        chmod "$4" "$target"
        chown "$1:$1" "$target"
      SH
      user.name, f.path, f.content, f.mode.to_s(8)
    ])
  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    Rails.logger.warn("inject_files: #{f.path} failed: #{e.message}")
  end
end
```

Call it from `create_container_and_start` right after `set_smb_password` (line 103).

## Seed entries

For the Claude/Codex case specifically, you'd seed two `InjectedFile` rows per user (created via a settings UI "paste your credentials" flow, or via a one-time host-side OAuth):

| path | mode | content |
|---|---|---|
| `.claude/.credentials.json` | 0600 | `{...}` |
| `.codex/auth.json` | 0600 | `{...}` |

Plus arbitrary user-defined files (`.npmrc`, `.config/gh/hosts.yml`, an `.env`) via the same UI.

## Recommendation

Build the generic `InjectedFile` mechanism — it's clearly useful beyond credentials. **For the Claude OAuth case specifically, still pair it with the bind-mount** of `~/.claude/` so refresh-token rotation doesn't bite. For everything else (API keys, dotfiles, gh tokens, .npmrc), injection is the cleaner path.

In other words: injection covers ~80% of "files I want in every sandbox." Bind-mount covers the remaining 20% where the file is also written-back inside the sandbox. The two mechanisms compose well.

> AGENT

## The categorization rubric

The rule of thumb: **does the sandbox write back to this file?** If no → inject. If yes → bind.

| File | Mechanism | Why |
|---|---|---|
| `.ssh/authorized_keys` | inject (already done) | sshd reads, never writes |
| `.ssh/id_ed25519`, `.ssh/config` | inject | static, set on the host |
| `.gitconfig` | inject (already done via env) | template from user profile |
| `.npmrc`, `.config/gh/hosts.yml` (PAT) | inject | static token strings |
| `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `OPENAI_API_KEY` | inject as env var | not even a file |
| `.aws/credentials` (static keys) | inject | static |
| `.vimrc`, `.tmux.conf`, `.zshrc` | inject | dotfiles, never rewritten |
| **`.claude/.credentials.json`** | **bind** | OAuth refresh rewrites it |
| **`.codex/auth.json`** | **bind** | same |
| `.config/gh/hosts.yml` (after `gh auth refresh`) | bind | gh rewrites it |
| `.aws/sso/cache/`, `.config/gcloud/` | bind | SSO rewrites tokens hourly |
| `.kube/config` (with exec plugins refreshing tokens) | bind | rewritten |
| `.cache/**`, `.npm/_cacache/**`, `node_modules/` | **neither** | rebuildable, large, noisy |

If you're unsure: bind is the safer default (writes round-trip), inject is the cheaper default (no mount, no UID-mapping headaches).

## How you add files to each

**Inject** — UI lives in Settings → "Files & Secrets":
- One-row form per file: `path` (relative to `$HOME`), `content` (textarea or file picker), `mode` (defaults 0600).
- Plus a separate "Environment variables" section for things like API keys.
- Backed by `injected_files` table (encrypted `content`), pushed into every new sandbox via `docker exec` after start (the SMB-password pattern, not env vars — keeps secrets out of `docker inspect`).

**Bind** — UI in the same screen, "Persisted directories":
- One-row form: `path` (relative to `$HOME`, e.g. `.claude`).
- Backed by `persisted_paths` table; `SandboxManager#volume_binds` translates each row into `/data/users/{name}/persisted/{path} : /home/{name}/{path}`.
- Created on the host with the existing `prepare_bind_mount` helper.

## How you do the *first* Claude login

This is the actual UX question. Two options:

**Option 1 — log in inside a sandbox, then promote:**
1. Start a sandbox with `~/.claude` already bind-mounted to `/data/users/{name}/persisted/.claude` (empty dir on first run).
2. Run `claude login` inside the sandbox → OAuth flow → credential file lands in the bind-mounted dir.
3. From now on every sandbox that mounts `.claude` is logged in.

This requires `.claude` to be in your "Persisted directories" list *before* the first login. Pre-seed the default list with `.claude` and `.codex` for every new user — zero clicks.

**Option 2 — host-side OAuth:** Settings page has a "Log in to Claude" button that runs the OAuth flow in the *web app*, captures the credential JSON, and writes it as either an `InjectedFile` row or directly into `/data/users/{name}/persisted/.claude/.credentials.json`. Slicker but requires implementing the OAuth client server-side and tracking refresh-token rotation there. Skip for v1.

Go with Option 1.

## The "what new files appeared?" discovery GUI

This is the missing piece — letting you discover that, say, `claude login` also dropped `~/.claude/projects/...` and `~/.claude/statsig/...` so you can decide whether they matter.

**Mechanism: snapshot-diff on demand.** No watchers, no inotify, just a button.

1. **Baseline at container start.** `entrypoint.sh` runs once after user creation:
   ```bash
   find /home/"$USERNAME" -xdev -type f -printf '%P\n' \
     | sort > /var/sandcastle/home-baseline.txt
   ```
   (Path-only baseline — cheap, no content hashing needed.)

2. **Sandbox detail page in the web UI** gets a "Discover new files" button. Clicking it does:
   ```ruby
   container.exec(["bash","-c", <<~SH])
     find /home/"$USER" -xdev -type f -printf '%P\n' \
       | sort \
       | comm -23 - /var/sandcastle/home-baseline.txt
   SH
   ```
   Returns a list of paths created or persisted since boot.

3. **Filter noise server-side.** Hard-coded ignore globs:
   ```
   .bash_history, .zsh_history, .lesshst, .python_history,
   .cache/**, .npm/_cacache/**, .local/share/Trash/**,
   .Xauthority, .ssh/known_hosts, .gnupg/random_seed,
   node_modules/**, .DS_Store, .vscode-server/**
   ```

4. **Render a 3-column table** in the UI:

   | Path | Suggested action | Action |
   |---|---|---|
   | `.claude/.credentials.json` | Bind (OAuth, rewritten) | [Bind] [Inject] [Ignore] |
   | `.claude/settings.json` | Inject (config, static) | [Bind] [Inject] [Ignore] |
   | `.claude/projects/foo.json` | Bind (per-project state) | [Bind] [Inject] [Ignore] |
   | `.config/gh/hosts.yml` | Inject (token, static) | [Bind] [Inject] [Ignore] |

   Suggestion is rule-based — pattern-match the path against an allowlist (`auto_suggest_action` helper). User can override.

5. **Clicking [Bind]** creates a `PersistedPath` row (path = parent dir, e.g. `.claude`). On next sandbox start, that dir is bind-mounted from `/data/users/{name}/persisted/.claude` — and the existing file is copied over from the current sandbox into that host dir as part of the action (so you don't lose what's already there).

6. **Clicking [Inject]** reads the file content via `container.exec` (`cat`), creates an `InjectedFile` row with the content + mode, and the next sandbox gets it dropped in via the post-start exec.

7. **Clicking [Ignore]** writes the path into a per-user `ignored_paths` set so it doesn't show up in future scans.

## Suggested build order

1. `InjectedFile` model + post-start `inject_files` exec + Settings UI for manual entry.
2. `PersistedPath` model + dynamic `volume_binds` from rows + Settings UI for manual entry. Pre-seed `.claude`, `.codex` for new users.
3. Baseline-snapshot in `entrypoint.sh`.
4. "Discover new files" button on the sandbox page with the diff/suggest/promote flow.

Steps 1–2 unlock the Claude/Codex login workflow today. Steps 3–4 turn it into a self-service "ratchet" — every sandbox session can teach Sandcastle a little more about which dotfiles you care about.

## Comprehensive Sandcastle Rails Conventions Briefing

### 1. USER MODEL (`app/models/user.rb`) — FULL SOURCE

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/models/user.rb` (Lines 1-135)

**Key Points:**

- **Encryption:** Uses `encrypts :smb_password` with Active Record Encryption. Includes a custom reader that rescues decryption errors and returns `nil` (lines 5-9), allowing graceful handling of unset/corrupted values.

- **Has-Many Associations:**
  - `has_many :sessions, dependent: :destroy`
  - `has_many :sandboxes, dependent: :destroy`
  - `has_many :api_tokens, dependent: :destroy`
  - `has_many :oauth_identities, dependent: :destroy`

- **Normalizations:**
  - `email_address`: strips and downcases
  - `name`: strips and downcases

- **Validations:**
  - `name`: presence, uniqueness, format `/\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{1,30}\z/` (2-31 lowercase alphanumeric, starts with letter)
  - `email_address`: presence, uniqueness
  - `status`: inclusion in `["active", "suspended", "pending_approval"]`
  - `sandbox_archive_retention_days`: optional, integer >= 0
  - `terminal_emulator`: optional, inclusion in `["ghostty", "xterm"]`
  - Custom validators: `validate_custom_links` (lines 119-133), `validate_ssh_keys` (lines 102-117)

- **SSH Keys Storage:**
  - Stored as JSONB array in `ssh_keys` column (added in migration `20260416000002`)
  - Each entry is a hash: `{ "name" => "...", "key" => "..." }`
  - Validates each entry: must have `"key"` field, must match SSH public key regex `/\A(ssh-|ecdsa-|sk-)\S+ \S+/`
  - Falls back to deprecated `ssh_public_key` text field in `all_ssh_keys_text` method (lines 71-78)

- **Custom Links Storage:**
  - Stored as JSONB array in `custom_links` column (added in migration `20260416000001`)
  - Each entry: `{ "name" => "...", "url" => "...", "show_on" => "all"|"desktop"|"tablet"|"phone" }`
  - Constants: `CUSTOM_LINK_SHOW_ON_VALUES`, `CUSTOM_LINK_TEMPLATE_VARS`, `TMUX_CMD`
  - URL template expansion method: `expand_custom_link_url` (lines 84-98) supports `{user}`, `{hostname}`, `{ssh_port}`, `{sandbox}`, `{tailscale_ip}`, `{tmux_cmd}`, `{tmux_cmd_encoded}`

- **Tailscale Wiring:**
  - Boolean flag: `tailscale_auto_connect` (defaults to false)
  - State field: `tailscale_state` (string, defaults to "disabled", can be "disabled"/"pending"/"enabled")
  - Predicate methods: `tailscale_enabled?`, `tailscale_pending?`, `tailscale_disabled?`, `tailscale_auto_connect?`, `chrome_persist_profile?`

- **Chrome Persist Profile:**
  - Boolean column `chrome_persist_profile` (defaults to true in schema, line 178)
  - Simple getter method `chrome_persist_profile?` (line 62)

---

### 2. SETTINGS CONTROLLER (`app/controllers/settings_controller.rb`) — FULL SOURCE

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/settings_controller.rb` (Lines 1-112)

**Architecture:** Separate action per feature, not a single `update` action.

**Actions:**

1. **`show` (GET /settings)**
   - Renders settings view with `@user` and `@api_tokens` (active, ordered by created_at desc)

2. **`update_profile` (PATCH /settings/update_profile)**
   - Permitted params: `:email_address`, `:full_name`, `:github_username`, `:chrome_persist_profile`, `:sandbox_archive_retention_days`, `:terminal_emulator`
   - Redirects to `settings_path` with notice or renders `:show` with `:unprocessable_entity` on errors

3. **`update_password` (PATCH /settings/update_password)**
   - Authenticates `current_password` first
   - Permitted params: `:password`, `:password_confirmation`
   - Redirects to `settings_path` with notice/alert

4. **`update_smb_password` (PATCH /settings/update_smb_password)**
   - Permitted params: `:smb_password`
   - Calls `SandboxManager.new.update_smb_password(user: @user)` after successful update
   - Redirects to `settings_path` with notice/alert

5. **`toggle_tailscale` (PATCH /settings/toggle_tailscale)**
   - Toggles `tailscale_auto_connect` boolean
   - Sets flash status: "enabled" or "disabled"
   - No params validation

6. **`update_custom_links` (PATCH /settings/update_custom_links)**
   - Params: `custom_links[]` (array of hashes: `:name`, `:url`, `:show_on`)
   - Rejects blank entries and normalizes/defaults `show_on` to "all"
   - Transforms: `{ "name" => l[:name].strip, "url" => l[:url].strip, "show_on" => l[:show_on].presence || "all" }`

7. **`update_ssh_keys` (PATCH /settings/update_ssh_keys)**
   - Params: `ssh_keys[]` (array of hashes: `:name`, `:key`)
   - Rejects blank keys, auto-generates `name` as `"key-#{SecureRandom.hex(3)}"` if blank
   - Transforms: `{ "name" => k[:name].strip.presence || ..., "key" => k[:key].strip }`

8. **`generate_token` (POST /settings/generate_token)**
   - Param: `:name` (defaults to "Web UI Token")
   - Calls `ApiToken.generate_for(@user, name: token_name)` → returns `[token, raw_token]`
   - Stores raw token in `flash[:api_token]` (single-use, shown in view)
   - Redirects with warning: "Make sure to copy it now - you won't be able to see it again!"

9. **`revoke_token` (DELETE /settings/revoke_token/:id)**
   - Finds token by `@user.api_tokens.find(params[:id])` and destroys it
   - Redirects with notice including token name

**Pattern:** Each action calls `@user = Current.user` and handles redirect with notice/alert. No bulk param permitting.

---

### 3. SETTINGS VIEW (`app/views/settings/show.html.erb`) — STRUCTURE

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/views/settings/show.html.erb` (Lines 1-435)

**Layout:** 10 distinct white-bordered sections within `max-w-3xl` container, Tailwind CSS styling.

**Sections (in order):**

1. **API Token Flash** (lines 4-15): Blue highlight box, copy button, one-time display
2. **Profile** (lines 17-55): 
   - Username (read-only text display)
   - `form_with` to `update_profile_settings_path`: `full_name`, `email_address`, `github_username`
   - Submit button updates profile only (not tailscale, terminal, chrome settings)
3. **Change Password** (lines 57-81):
   - `form_with` to `update_password_settings_path`: `current_password`, `user[password]`, `user[password_confirmation]`
   - Direct password_field_tag (not model-bound)
4. **Tailscale** (lines 83-132):
   - Status indicator with dot (green/yellow/gray)
   - Toggle form: auto-connect checkbox with `onchange="this.form.requestSubmit()"`
   - Conditional "Manage Tailscale" link if enabled
5. **VNC** (lines 134-151):
   - Chrome persist profile checkbox (part of `update_profile_settings_path`)
   - Auto-submit on change
6. **Terminal** (lines 153-170):
   - Terminal emulator select dropdown (part of `update_profile_settings_path`)
   - Options: "Ghostty Web" (ghostty), "xterm.js" (xterm)
   - Auto-submit on change
7. **Custom Links** (lines 172-251):
   - Stimulus controller: `data-controller="custom-links"`
   - Template-based dynamic rows with name, URL, show_on dropdown
   - Uses `<template data-custom-links-target="template">` and cloning
   - Add button clones template, remove button deletes rows
   - Form submits to `update_custom_links_settings_path`
8. **API Tokens** (lines 253-315):
   - Token generation form (initially hidden, toggled by JS)
   - Token list with name, prefix code, created/last-used timestamps
   - Revoke button (DELETE, with confirmation message)
   - Tip box with CLI reference
9. **Sandbox Archive** (lines 317-340):
   - Number field for retention days with system default fallback
   - Form to `update_profile_settings_path`
10. **File Sharing (SMB)** (lines 342-374):
    - Password field for SMB access
    - Mount instructions for macOS/Windows/Linux
    - Form to `update_smb_password_settings_path`
11. **SSH Keys** (lines 376-434):
    - Stimulus controller: `data-controller="ssh-keys"`
    - Template-based dynamic rows with name label and key textarea
    - Key display shows first word and last 12 chars (truncated)
    - Add/remove buttons, form to `update_ssh_keys_settings_path`

**Form Patterns:**
- Most use `form_with model: @user, url: update_*_settings_path`
- Some use `form_with url: ...` (custom links, SSH keys) to handle nested array params
- `onchange="this.form.requestSubmit()"` for immediate auto-submit (toggles, dropdowns)

**Stimulus Controllers:**
- `custom-links` and `ssh-keys` share identical pattern:
  - Targets: `list` (container), `template` (for cloning)
  - Actions: `add(event)`, `remove(event)`
  - Template rows identified by `data-custom-links-row` or `data-ssh-keys-row`

### 4. SANDBOX MODEL (`app/models/sandbox.rb`) — SCHEMA & FLAGS

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/models/sandbox.rb` (Lines 1-110)

**Key Flags:**

- **`mount_home`** (boolean, default: false) — Line 106 in schema. When true, sandbox home directory is mounted/persisted across recreations.

- **`data_path`** (string, nullable) — Line 97 in schema. Path for data volume mounting. Often corresponds to a mounted volume within the container.

- **`persistent_volume`** (boolean, default: false) — Line 108. Whether a separate persistent volume is used.

- **Other relevant boolean flags:**
  - `docker_enabled` (default: true)
  - `vnc_enabled` (default: true)
  - `temporary` (default: false) — soft-delete marker
  - `smb_enabled` (default: false)
  - `tailscale` (default: false)

**Validations (lines 11-19):**
- `name`: presence, uniqueness scoped to user (excludes destroyed/archived)
- `name`: format `/\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}\z/` (unless destroyed/archived)
- `status`: inclusion in `["pending", "running", "stopped", "destroyed", "archived"]`
- `image`: presence
- `vnc_geometry`: inclusion in `VNC_GEOMETRIES` (1280x900, 1366x768, 1440x900, 1600x900, 1920x1080, 2560x1440)
- `vnc_depth`: inclusion in `VNC_DEPTHS` ([8, 16, 24, 32])
- Custom validator: `smb_prerequisites` (line 19) — requires Tailscale enabled and SMB password set if smb_enabled

**Associations (lines 4-6):**
- `belongs_to :user`
- `has_many :routes, dependent: :destroy`
- `has_many :container_metrics, dependent: :delete_all`

**Turbo Broadcasts (lines 26-28):**
- `after_create_commit :broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard`
- `after_update_commit :broadcast_replace_to_dashboard`
- `after_destroy_commit :broadcast_remove_from_dashboard`

**Job Lifecycle Methods (lines 51-69):**
- `job_in_progress?`, `job_failed?`, `start_job(status)`, `finish_job`, `fail_job(error_message)`

**`test/test_helper.rb` (Lines 1-21):**

```ruby
ENV["RAILS_ENV"] ||= "test"
```

```ruby
require "test_helper"

class UserTest < ActiveSupport::TestCase
  test "downcases and strips email_address" do
    user = User.new(email_address: " DOWNCASED@EXAMPLE.COM ")
    assert_equal("downcased@example.com", user.email_address)
  end
end
```

**`test/controllers/admin/settings_controller_test.rb` (Lines 1-67):**
- Inherits from `ActionDispatch::IntegrationTest`
- Setup: `sign_in_as(@admin)` using fixtures (`users(:one)`, `users(:two)`)
- Tests admin access, OAuth settings update, SMTP settings update
- Pattern: `patch admin_settings_path, params: { setting: { ... } }`
- Assertions: `assert_redirected_to`, `assert_equal`, `assert_response :success`

**Session Helper (`test/test_helpers/session_test_helper.rb`):**
- `sign_in_as(user)`: Creates session, sets signed cookie
- `sign_out`: Destroys session, deletes cookie
- Auto-mixed into `ActionDispatch::IntegrationTest`

**Docker Mock (`test/support/docker_mock.rb`, lines 1-100):**
- Singleton module with class methods: `reset!`, `enable!`, `inject_failure(type)`
- Prepends mock modules to `Docker::Container`, `Docker::Image`, `Docker::Network`
- Mock methods:
  - `Docker::Container.create(opts)` → returns mock container with ID
  - `Docker::Container.get(id)` → retrieves from `DockerMock.containers` hash
  - `Docker::Container.all(opts, conn)` → lists all containers
  - Instance methods: `info`, `start`, `stop`, `remove`
- Mocks return `Docker::Base` instances with `@info` set via `DockerMock.build(klass, id, info)`
- Failure injection: `DockerMock.inject_failure(:create)` raises `Docker::Error::DockerError`
- Used in test setup: `DockerMock.reset!` before each test

**SandboxManager Test Pattern (`test/services/sandbox_manager_test.rb`, lines 1-80):**
```ruby
class SandboxManagerTest < ActiveSupport::TestCase
  setup do
    @manager = SandboxManager.new
    @user = users(:one)
    @sandbox = sandboxes(:alice_running)
    DockerMock.reset!
  end

  test "create_container_and_start creates and starts container" do
    @sandbox.update!(container_id: nil, status: "pending")
    @manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: @sandbox, user: @user)
    @sandbox.reload
    assert_not_nil @sandbox.container_id
    assert_equal "running", @sandbox.status
    container = Docker::Container.get(@sandbox.container_id)
    assert_equal "running", container.info["State"]["Status"]
  end
end
```

```ruby
  def up
    add_column :users, :ssh_keys, :jsonb, default: []

      SET ssh_keys = jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('name', 'default', 'key', ssh_public_key))

    SQL
```

### 7. ACTIVE RECORD ENCRYPTION

**Configuration:**

- **Test** (`config/environments/test.rb`, lines 54-58):
  ```ruby
  config.active_record.encryption.primary_key = "REDACTED"
  config.active_record.encryption.deterministic_key = "test_deterministic_key_0123456789"
  config.active_record.encryption.key_derivation_salt = "test_derivation_salt_0123456789ab"
  ```

- **Production:** Uses default Rails mechanism (reads from credentials store + `config/master.key`)
  - File exists: `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/config/master.key`
  - Credentials file: `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/config/credentials.yml.enc`
  - No explicit `config.active_record.encryption.*` in production config; relies on defaults

**Usage Pattern:**
```ruby
class User < ApplicationRecord
  encrypts :smb_password

  def smb_password
    super
  rescue ActiveRecord::Encryption::Errors::Decryption
    nil
  end
end
```

**OAuth Credentials Storage:** Ready for `encrypts :oauth_token_field` pattern. Rails 8.1 automatically uses `config/master.key` + credentials file in production; test config has hardcoded keys for determinism across parallel workers.

### KEY CONVENTIONS SUMMARY FOR FEATURE IMPLEMENTATION

1. **Separate actions per feature** in controllers (not one giant `update`)
2. **JSONB columns** for structured data (custom_links, ssh_keys) with defaults `[]`
3. **`encrypts :field` keyword** for sensitive fields; custom reader to rescue decryption errors
4. **Stimulus controllers** for dynamic forms (template cloning, add/remove buttons)
5. **Validation in model** with custom validators for complex rules
6. **Test fixtures** with ERB for dynamic values (password digest)
7. **Integration tests** using `ActionDispatch::IntegrationTest` with `sign_in_as` helper
8. **DockerMock** for service tests; stateful mock with `.reset!` before each test
9. **Migrations use `add_column` with explicit types/defaults**; data migrations via `execute`
10. **Flash messages** for success/error; form errors via `@model.errors[:field]`
11. **Form patterns**: `form_with model: @user` for model forms, `form_with url: path` for custom params
12. **Tailwind CSS** with utility classes; sections in `max-w-3xl` containers; consistent spacing
```

1	class User < ApplicationRecord
2	  has_secure_password
3	  encrypts :smb_password
4	
5	  def smb_password
6	    super
7	  rescue ActiveRecord::Encryption::Errors::Decryption
8	    nil
9	  end
10	  has_many :sessions, dependent: :destroy
11	  has_many :sandboxes, dependent: :destroy
12	  has_many :api_tokens, dependent: :destroy
13	  has_many :oauth_identities, dependent: :destroy
14	
15	  normalizes :email_address, with: ->(e) { e.strip.downcase }
16	  normalizes :name, with: ->(n) { n.strip.downcase }
17	
18	  validates :name, presence: true, uniqueness: true,
19	    format: { with: /\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{1,30}\z/, message: "must be lowercase alphanumeric (2-31 chars, start with letter)" }
20	  validates :email_address, presence: true, uniqueness: true
21	  validates :status, inclusion: { in: %w[active suspended pending_approval] }
22	  validates :sandbox_archive_retention_days, numericality: { only_integer: true, greater_than_or_equal_to: 0, allow_nil: true }
23	  validates :terminal_emulator, inclusion: { in: %w[ghostty xterm], allow_nil: true }
24	  validate :validate_custom_links
25	  validate :validate_ssh_keys
26	
27	  scope :active, -> { where(status: "active") }
28	
29	  def admin?
30	    admin
31	  end
32	
33	  def active?
34	    status == "active"
35	  end
36	
37	  def suspended?
38	    status == "suspended"
39	  end
40	
41	  def pending_approval?
42	    status == "pending_approval"
43	  end
44	
45	  def tailscale_enabled?
46	    respond_to?(:tailscale_state) && tailscale_state == "enabled"
47	  end
48	
49	  def tailscale_pending?
50	    respond_to?(:tailscale_state) && tailscale_state == "pending"
51	  end
52	
53	  def tailscale_disabled?
54	    !respond_to?(:tailscale_state) || tailscale_state == "disabled"
55	  end
56	
57	  def tailscale_auto_connect?
58	    tailscale_auto_connect
59	  end
60	
61	  def chrome_persist_profile?
62	    chrome_persist_profile
63	  end
64	
65	  def effective_archive_retention_days
66	    return sandbox_archive_retention_days if sandbox_archive_retention_days.present?
67	
68	    @effective_archive_retention_days_fallback ||= Setting.instance.sandbox_archive_retention_days || 30
69	  end
70	
71	  def all_ssh_keys_text
72	    keys = ssh_keys.presence&.filter_map { |k| k["key"].presence }
73	    if keys.present?
74	      keys.join("\n")
75	    else
76	      ssh_public_key
77	    end
78	  end
79	
80	  CUSTOM_LINK_SHOW_ON_VALUES = %w[all desktop tablet phone].freeze
81	  CUSTOM_LINK_TEMPLATE_VARS = %w[user hostname ssh_port sandbox tailscale_ip tmux_cmd tmux_cmd_encoded].freeze
82	  TMUX_CMD = "tmux new-session -A -s main".freeze
83	
84	  def expand_custom_link_url(url_template, sandbox:, tailscale_ip: nil)
85	    hostname = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
86	    url_template.gsub(/\{(\w+)\}/) do |match|
87	      case $1
88	      when "user" then name
89	      when "hostname" then hostname
90	      when "ssh_port" then sandbox.ssh_port.to_s
91	      when "sandbox" then sandbox.name
92	      when "tailscale_ip" then tailscale_ip.to_s
93	      when "tmux_cmd" then TMUX_CMD
94	      when "tmux_cmd_encoded" then ERB::Util.url_encode(TMUX_CMD)
95	      else match
96	      end
97	    end
98	  end
99	
100	  private
101	
102	  def validate_ssh_keys
103	    return if ssh_keys.blank?
104	    unless ssh_keys.is_a?(Array)
105	      errors.add(:ssh_keys, "must be an array")
106	      return
107	    end
108	    ssh_keys.each_with_index do |entry, i|
109	      unless entry.is_a?(Hash) && entry["key"].present?
110	        errors.add(:ssh_keys, "entry #{i + 1} must have a key")
111	        next
112	      end
113	      unless entry["key"].match?(/\A(ssh-|ecdsa-|sk-)\S+ \S+/)
114	        errors.add(:ssh_keys, "entry #{i + 1} does not look like a valid SSH public key")
115	      end
116	    end
117	  end
118	
119	  def validate_custom_links
120	    return if custom_links.blank?
121	    unless custom_links.is_a?(Array)
122	      errors.add(:custom_links, "must be an array")
123	      return
124	    end
125	    custom_links.each_with_index do |link, i|
126	      unless link.is_a?(Hash) && link["name"].present? && link["url"].present?
127	        errors.add(:custom_links, "entry #{i + 1} must have a name and url")
128	      end
129	      if link["show_on"].present? && !CUSTOM_LINK_SHOW_ON_VALUES.include?(link["show_on"])
130	        errors.add(:custom_links, "entry #{i + 1} show_on must be one of: #{CUSTOM_LINK_SHOW_ON_VALUES.join(', ')}")
131	      end
132	    end
133	  end
134	end
```

```
1	class SettingsController < ApplicationController
2	  def show
3	    @user = Current.user
4	    @api_tokens = @user.api_tokens.active.order(created_at: :desc)
5	  end
6	
7	  def update_profile
8	    @user = Current.user
9	
10	    if @user.update(profile_params)
11	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "Profile updated successfully."
12	    else
13	      render :show, status: :unprocessable_entity
14	    end
15	  end
16	
17	  def update_password
18	    @user = Current.user
19	
20	    unless @user.authenticate(params[:current_password])
21	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: "Current password is incorrect."
22	      return
23	    end
24	
25	    if @user.update(password_params)
26	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "Password changed successfully."
27	    else
28	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(", ")
29	    end
30	  end
31	
32	  def update_smb_password
33	    @user = Current.user
34	
35	    if @user.update(smb_password_params)
36	      SandboxManager.new.update_smb_password(user: @user)
37	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "SMB password updated."
38	    else
39	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(", ").presence || "Failed to update SMB password."
40	    end
41	  end
42	
43	  def toggle_tailscale
44	    @user = Current.user
45	
46	    if @user.update(tailscale_auto_connect: !@user.tailscale_auto_connect)
47	      status = @user.tailscale_auto_connect ? "enabled" : "disabled"
48	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "Tailscale auto-connect #{status}."
49	    else
50	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: "Failed to update Tailscale settings."
51	    end
52	  end
53	
54	  def update_custom_links
55	    @user = Current.user
56	    links = (params[:custom_links] || []).reject { |l| l[:name].blank? && l[:url].blank? }.map do |l|
57	      { "name" => l[:name].to_s.strip, "url" => l[:url].to_s.strip, "show_on" => l[:show_on].presence || "all" }
58	    end
59	
60	    if @user.update(custom_links: links)
61	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "Custom links updated."
62	    else
63	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(", ")
64	    end
65	  end
66	
67	  def update_ssh_keys
68	    @user = Current.user
69	    keys = (params[:ssh_keys] || []).reject { |k| k[:key].blank? }.map do |k|
70	      { "name" => k[:name].to_s.strip.presence || "key-#{SecureRandom.hex(3)}", "key" => k[:key].to_s.strip }
71	    end
72	
73	    if @user.update(ssh_keys: keys)
74	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "SSH keys updated."
75	    else
76	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(", ")
77	    end
78	  end
79	
80	  def generate_token
81	    @user = Current.user
82	
83	    token_name = params[:name].presence || "Web UI Token"
84	    token, raw_token = ApiToken.generate_for(@user, name: token_name)
85	
86	    flash[:api_token] = raw_token
87	    redirect_to settings_path, notice: "API token '#{token_name}' generated. Make sure to copy it now - you won't be able to see it again!"
88	  end
89	
90	  def revoke_token
91	    @user = Current.user
92	    token = @user.api_tokens.find(params[:id])
93	    token_name = token.name
94	    token.destroy!
95	
96	    redirect_to settings_path, notice: "API token '#{token_name}' revoked."
97	  end
98	
99	  private
100	
101	  def profile_params
102	    params.require(:user).permit(:email_address, :full_name, :github_username, :chrome_persist_profile, :sandbox_archive_retention_days, :terminal_emulator)
103	  end
104	
105	  def password_params
106	    params.require(:user).permit(:password, :password_confirmation)
107	  end
108	
109	  def smb_password_params
110	    params.require(:user).permit(:smb_password)
111	  end
112	end
113
```

```
174	    <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-2">Custom Links</h2>
175	    <p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">
176	      Add custom links that appear on each sandbox card. Use template variables in the URL:
177	      <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-xs">{user}</code>,
178	      <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-xs">{hostname}</code>,
179	      <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-xs">{ssh_port}</code>,
180	      <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-xs">{sandbox}</code>,
181	      <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-xs">{tailscale_ip}</code>,
182	      <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-xs">{tmux_cmd}</code>,
183	      <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-xs">{tmux_cmd_encoded}</code>
184	    </p>

195	            </div>

203	                <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M10 18a8 8 0 1 0 0-16 8 8 0 0 0 0 16ZM8.28 7.22a.75.75 0 0 0-1.06 1.06L8.94 10l-1.72 1.72a.75.75 0 1 0 1.06 1.06L10 11.06l1.72 1.72a.75.75 0 1 0 1.06-1.06L11.06 10l1.72-1.72a.75.75 0 0 0-1.06-1.06L10 8.94 8.28 7.22Z" clip-rule="evenodd" />
204	              </svg>

209

214	                   class="w-full px-2 py-1.5 border border-gray-300 rounded text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 mb-1">

245	    <div class="mt-4 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded p-3 text-xs text-gray-600 space-y-1">
246	      <p><strong>Example:</strong> <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono">laterminal://open/{user}@{hostname}:{ssh_port}?command={tmux_cmd_encoded}</code> — open sandbox in LaTerminal with tmux</p>
247	      <p><strong>Example:</strong> <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono">ssh://{user}@{hostname}:{ssh_port}</code> — open plain SSH connection</p>
248	      <p><strong><code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono">{tmux_cmd}</code></strong> expands to <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono"><%= User::TMUX_CMD %></code> (plain text). <strong><code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono">{tmux_cmd_encoded}</code></strong> is the URL-encoded version (for query parameters).</p>
249	      <p><strong>Show on:</strong> controls which devices display the link — <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono">all</code>, <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono">desktop</code>, <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono">tablet</code>, or <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono">phone</code></p>
250	    </div>

253	  <%# ── API Tokens Section ── %>
254	  <div class="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-6 mb-6">
255	    <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
256	      <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">API Tokens</h2>
257	      <button onclick="document.getElementById('new-token-form').classList.toggle('hidden')"
258	              class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded hover:bg-blue-700 text-sm">
259	        New Token
260	      </button>
261	    </div>
262	
263	    <%# New Token Form (hidden by default) %>
264	    <div id="new-token-form" class="hidden mb-4 p-4 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg">
265	      <%= form_with url: generate_token_settings_path, method: :post, class: "flex gap-2" do |f| %>
266	        <%= text_field_tag :name, nil, placeholder: "Token name (e.g., 'laptop', 'CI/CD')",
267	              class: "flex-1 px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500" %>
268	        <%= button_tag "Generate", type: :submit,
269	              class: "px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded hover:bg-blue-700 text-sm whitespace-nowrap" %>
270	        <button type="button"
271	                onclick="document.getElementById('new-token-form').classList.add('hidden')"
272	                class="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-300 text-gray-700 rounded hover:bg-gray-400 text-sm">
273	          Cancel
274	        </button>
275	      <% end %>
276	    </div>
277	
278	    <% if @api_tokens.any? %>
279	      <div class="space-y-3">
280	        <% @api_tokens.each do |token| %>
281	          <div class="flex items-start justify-between p-3 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg">
282	            <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
283	              <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
284	                <p class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900"><%= token.name %></p>
285	                <code class="text-xs bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-mono">
286	                  <%= token.prefix %>
287	                </code>
288	              </div>
289	              <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">
290	                Created <%= time_ago_in_words(token.created_at) %> ago
291	                <% if token.last_used_at %>
292	                  · Last used <%= time_ago_in_words(token.last_used_at) %> ago
293	                <% else %>
294	                  · Never used
295	                <% end %>
296	              </p>
297	            </div>
298	            <%= button_to "Revoke", revoke_token_settings_path(id: token.id), method: :delete,
299	                  class: "ml-3 px-3 py-1.5 bg-red-600 text-white rounded hover:bg-red-700 text-xs whitespace-nowrap",
300	                  data: { confirm_message: "Revoke '#{token.name}'? Applications using this token will stop working." } %>
301	          </div>
302	        <% end %>
303	      </div>
304	    <% else %>
305	      <div class="space-y-4">
306	        <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">No API tokens generated yet. Create one to use the Sandcastle CLI and API.</p>
307	      </div>
308	    <% end %>
309	
310	    <div class="mt-4 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4">
311	      <p class="text-xs text-blue-800">
312	        <strong>Tip:</strong> Use your API token with the Sandcastle CLI by running <code class="bg-blue-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-mono">sandcastle token create</code>
313	      </p>
314	    </div>
315	  </div>
316	
317	  <%# ── Sandbox Archive Section ── %>
318	  <div class="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-6 mb-6">
319	    <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-4">Sandbox Archive</h2>
320	    <% default_retention_days = Setting.instance.sandbox_archive_retention_days || 30 %>
321	    <p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">
322	      When you remove a sandbox it is archived (soft-deleted) instead of immediately purged,
323	      so you can restore it if needed. Leave blank to use the system default
324	      (<%= default_retention_days %> days).
325	      Set to <strong>0</strong> to disable archiving (immediate purge on remove).
326	    </p>
327	
328	    <%= form_with model: @user, url: update_profile_settings_path, method: :patch, class: "flex items-end gap-3" do |f| %>
329	      <div>
330	        <%= f.label :sandbox_archive_retention_days, "Retention period (days)", class: "block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1" %>
331	        <%= f.number_field :sandbox_archive_retention_days,
332	              min: 0, max: 365,
333	              placeholder: "#{default_retention_days} (system default)",
334	              class: "w-40 px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500" %>
335	      </div>
336	      <button type="submit" class="px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded hover:bg-blue-700 text-sm">
337	        Save
338	      </button>
339	    <% end %>
340	  </div>

343	  <div class="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-6 mb-6">
344	    <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-4">File Sharing (SMB)</h2>
345	
346	    <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-4">
347	      Set an SMB password to mount your sandbox's home directory or /persisted as a network drive from macOS, Windows, or Linux.
348	      Enable SMB per-sandbox when creating or editing a sandbox. Requires Tailscale for access.
349	    </p>
350

352	      <div>
353	        <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">SMB Password</label>
354	        <%= password_field_tag "user[smb_password]", nil,
355	              placeholder: @user.smb_password.present? ? "••••••••" : "Set a password for SMB access",
356	              class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500" %>
357	        <% if @user.smb_password.present? %>
358	          <p class="text-xs text-green-600 mt-1">SMB password is set. Enter a new value to change it.</p>
359	        <% else %>
360	          <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">No SMB password set. SMB will not start even if enabled on a sandbox.</p>
361	        <% end %>
362	      </div>
363	
364	      <div class="bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded p-3 text-xs text-gray-600 space-y-1">
365	        <p><strong>macOS:</strong> <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono">smb://&lt;tailscale-ip&gt;/home</code></p>
366	        <p><strong>Windows:</strong> <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono">\\&lt;tailscale-ip&gt;\home</code></p>
367	        <p><strong>Linux:</strong> <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded font-mono">mount -t cifs //&lt;tailscale-ip&gt;/home /mnt -o username=&lt;you&gt;</code></p>
368	      </div>
369	
370	      <button type="submit" class="px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded hover:bg-blue-700 text-sm">
371	        Save SMB Password
372	      </button>
373	    <% end %>
374	  </div>
```
