1	# frozen_string_literal: true
2	
3	# Stateful Docker API mock for testing
4	# Simulates Docker container and image operations without requiring real Docker
5	module DockerMock
6	  class << self
7	    attr_accessor :containers, :images, :networks, :failure_mode
8	
9	    def reset!
10	      @containers = {}
11	      @images = {
12	        "busybox:latest" => {
13	          "Id" => "sha256:busybox_default",
14	          "RepoTags" => [ "busybox:latest" ],
15	          "Size" => 1_000_000,
16	          "Created" => 0
17	        }
18	      }
19	      @networks = {}
20	      @failure_mode = nil
21	    end
22	
23	    def enable!
24	      reset!
25	      setup_mocks
26	    end
27	
28	    def inject_failure(type)
29	      @failure_mode = type
30	    end
31	
32	    # Build a mock Docker object. Docker::Base.new is private and requires
33	    # a connection + hash with 'id'. We use send(:new) to bypass visibility.
34	    def build(klass, id, info = {})
35	      conn = Docker.connection
36	      obj = klass.send(:new, conn, { "id" => id }.merge(info))
37	      obj.instance_variable_set(:@info, info)
38	      obj
39	    end
40	
41	    private
42	
43	    def setup_mocks
44	      # Mock Docker::Container
45	      Docker::Container.singleton_class.prepend(ContainerMethods)
46	      Docker::Container.prepend(ContainerInstanceMethods)
47	
48	      # Mock Docker::Image
49	      Docker::Image.singleton_class.prepend(ImageMethods)
50	      Docker::Image.prepend(ImageInstanceMethods)
51	
52	      # Mock Docker::Network
53	      Docker::Network.singleton_class.prepend(NetworkMethods)
54	      Docker::Network.prepend(NetworkInstanceMethods)
55	    end
56	  end
57	
58	  module ContainerMethods
59	    def create(opts)
60	      raise Docker::Error::DockerError, "Simulated create failure" if DockerMock.failure_mode == :create
61	
62	      container_id = "mock_#{SecureRandom.hex(16)}"
63	      container_name = opts.dig("name") || "container_#{SecureRandom.hex(4)}"
64	
65	      container_data = {
66	        "Id" => container_id,
67	        "Name" => container_name,
68	        "State" => {
69	          "Status" => "created",
70	          "Running" => false,
71	          "Pid" => 0
72	        },
73	        "Config" => opts,
74	        "NetworkSettings" => {
75	          "Ports" => {},
76	          "Networks" => {}
77	        }
78	      }
79	
80	      DockerMock.containers[container_id] = container_data
81	      sleep 0.1 # Simulate creation delay
82	
83	      DockerMock.build(Docker::Container, container_id, container_data)
84	    end
85	
86	    def get(id)
87	      raise Docker::Error::NotFoundError, "Container not found" unless DockerMock.containers.key?(id)
88	
89	      container_data = DockerMock.containers[id]
90	      DockerMock.build(Docker::Container, id, container_data)
91	    end
92	
93	    def all(opts = {}, _conn = nil)
94	      DockerMock.containers.values.map do |data|
95	        DockerMock.build(Docker::Container, data["Id"], data)
96	      end
97	    end
98	  end
99	
100	  module ContainerInstanceMethods
101	    def start
102	      raise Docker::Error::DockerError, "Simulated start failure" if DockerMock.failure_mode == :start
103	
104	      container_data = DockerMock.containers[@id]
105	      return unless container_data
106	
107	      container_data["State"]["Status"] = "running"
108	      container_data["State"]["Running"] = true
109	      container_data["State"]["Pid"] = rand(1000..9999)
110	
111	      sleep 0.5 # Simulate start delay
112	      self
113	    end
114	
115	    def stop(opts = {})
116	      raise Docker::Error::DockerError, "Simulated stop failure" if DockerMock.failure_mode == :stop
117	
118	      container_data = DockerMock.containers[@id]
119	      return unless container_data
120	
121	      container_data["State"]["Status"] = "exited"
122	      container_data["State"]["Running"] = false
123	      container_data["State"]["Pid"] = 0
124	
125	      sleep 0.3 # Simulate stop delay
126	      self
127	    end
128	
129	    def delete(opts = {})
130	      raise Docker::Error::DockerError, "Simulated delete failure" if DockerMock.failure_mode == :delete
131	
132	      DockerMock.containers.delete(@id)
133	      sleep 0.2 # Simulate delete delay
134	      self
135	    end
136	
137	    def stats(opts = {})
138	      {
139	        "cpu_stats" => {

180	      self

185	    end

192	      json
193	    end
194	
195	    def commit(opts = {})
196	      image_id = "sha256:#{SecureRandom.hex(32)}"
197	      repo = opts[:repo] || opts["repo"] || "snapshot"
198	      tag = opts[:tag] || opts["tag"] || "latest"
199	
200	      image_data = {
201	        "Id" => image_id,
202	        "RepoTags" => [ "#{repo}:#{tag}" ],
203	        "Size" => rand(100_000_000..500_000_000),
204	        "Created" => Time.current.to_i
205	      }
206	
207	      DockerMock.images[image_id] = image_data
208	
209	      DockerMock.build(Docker::Image, image_id, image_data)
210	    end
211	  end
212	
213	  module ImageMethods
214	    def create(opts = {}, _creds = nil, _conn = nil)
215	      raise Docker::Error::DockerError, "Simulated pull failure" if DockerMock.failure_mode == :pull
216	
217	      from_image = opts["fromImage"] || "library/ubuntu:latest"
218	      image_id = "sha256:#{SecureRandom.hex(32)}"
219	
220	      image_data = {
221	        "Id" => image_id,
222	        "RepoTags" => [ from_image ],
223	        "Size" => rand(100_000_000..500_000_000),
224	        "Created" => Time.current.to_i
225	      }
226	
227	      DockerMock.images[image_id] = image_data
228	      sleep 1 # Simulate pull delay
229	
230	      DockerMock.build(Docker::Image, image_id, image_data)
231	    end
232	
233	    def all(opts = {})
234	      DockerMock.images.values.map do |data|
235	        DockerMock.build(Docker::Image, data["Id"], data)
236	      end
237	    end
238	
239	    def get(id_or_name, _opts = {}, _conn = nil)
240	      image_data = DockerMock.images[id_or_name] ||
241	                   DockerMock.images.values.find { |img| img["RepoTags"]&.include?(id_or_name) }
242	
243	      raise Docker::Error::NotFoundError, "Image not found" unless image_data
244	
245	      DockerMock.build(Docker::Image, image_data["Id"], image_data)
246	    end
247	  end
248	
249	  module ImageInstanceMethods
250	    def remove(opts = {})
251	      DockerMock.images.delete(@id)
252	      # Also remove by repo tag
253	      DockerMock.images.reject! { |_k, v| v["Id"] == @id }
254	    end
255	
256	    def info
257	      @info || {}
258	    end
259	  end
260	
261	  module NetworkMethods
262	    def create(name, opts = {})
263	      network_id = "mock_net_#{SecureRandom.hex(16)}"
264	
265	      network_data = {
266	        "Id" => network_id,
267	        "Name" => name,
268	        "Driver" => opts["Driver"] || "bridge",
269	        "Labels" => opts["Labels"] || {},
270	        "IPAM" => opts["IPAM"] || {},
271	        "Containers" => {}
272	      }
273	
274	      DockerMock.networks[network_id] = network_data
275	
276	      DockerMock.build(Docker::Network, network_id, network_data)
277	    end
278	
279	    def get(id_or_name)
280	      network_data = DockerMock.networks[id_or_name] ||
281	                     DockerMock.networks.values.find { |net| net["Name"] == id_or_name }
282	
283	      raise Docker::Error::NotFoundError, "Network not found" unless network_data
284	
285	      DockerMock.build(Docker::Network, network_data["Id"], network_data)
286	    end
287	
288	    def all(opts = {})
289	      DockerMock.networks.values.map do |data|
290	        DockerMock.build(Docker::Network, data["Id"], data)
291	      end
292	    end
293	  end
294	
295	  module NetworkInstanceMethods
296	    def connect(container_id, _opts = {})
297	      raise Docker::Error::NotFoundError, "Container not found" unless DockerMock.containers.key?(container_id)
298	
299	      network_data = DockerMock.networks.values.find { |n| n["Id"] == @id }
300	      network_data["Containers"][container_id] = {} if network_data
301	
302	      container_data = DockerMock.containers[container_id]
303	      if container_data
304	        container_data["NetworkSettings"] ||= {}
305	        container_data["NetworkSettings"]["Networks"] ||= {}
306	        container_data["NetworkSettings"]["Networks"][network_data&.dig("Name") || @id] = {}
307	      end
308	    end
309	
310	    def disconnect(container_id, _opts = {})
311	      network_data = DockerMock.networks.values.find { |n| n["Id"] == @id }
312	      network_data&.dig("Containers")&.delete(container_id)
313	
314	      container_data = DockerMock.containers[container_id]
315	      if container_data
316	        name = network_data&.dig("Name") || @id
317	        container_data.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks")&.delete(name)
318	      end
319	    end
320	
321	    def delete
322	      DockerMock.networks.delete(@id)
323	    end
324	
325	    def info
326	      @info || {}
327	    end
328	  end
329	end
330

3	require "test_helper"

5	class SandboxManagerTest < ActiveSupport::TestCase
6	  setup do
7	    @manager = SandboxManager.new
8	    @user = users(:one)
9	    @sandbox = sandboxes(:alice_running)
10	    DockerMock.reset!
11	  end
12	
13	  test "create_container_and_start creates and starts container" do
14	    @sandbox.update!(container_id: nil, status: "pending")
15	
16	    @manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: @sandbox, user: @user)
17	
18	    @sandbox.reload
19	    assert_not_nil @sandbox.container_id
20	    assert_equal "running", @sandbox.status
21	
22	    # Verify container exists in mock
23	    container = Docker::Container.get(@sandbox.container_id)
24	    assert_equal "running", container.info["State"]["Status"]
25	  end
26	
27	  test "start starts a stopped container" do
28	    # Create container first
29	    @sandbox.update!(container_id: nil, status: "pending")
30	    @manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: @sandbox, user: @user)
31	    container_id = @sandbox.container_id
32	
33	    # Stop it
34	    @manager.stop(sandbox: @sandbox)
35	    assert_equal "stopped", @sandbox.reload.status
36	
37	    # Start it again (creates a new container)
38	    @manager.start(sandbox: @sandbox)
39	    @sandbox.reload
40	    assert_equal "running", @sandbox.status
41	
42	    container = Docker::Container.get(@sandbox.container_id)
43	    assert container.info["State"]["Running"]
44	  end
45	
46	  test "stop stops a running container" do
47	    @sandbox.update!(container_id: nil, status: "pending")
48	    @manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: @sandbox, user: @user)
49	    assert_equal "running", @sandbox.reload.status
50	
51	    @manager.stop(sandbox: @sandbox)
52	    assert_equal "stopped", @sandbox.reload.status
53	
54	    container = Docker::Container.get(@sandbox.container_id)
55	    assert_not container.info["State"]["Running"]
56	  end
57	
58	  test "destroy removes container and updates status" do
59	    @sandbox.update!(container_id: nil, status: "pending")
60	    @manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: @sandbox, user: @user)
61	    container_id = @sandbox.container_id
62	
63	    @manager.destroy(sandbox: @sandbox)
64	
65	    assert_equal "destroyed", @sandbox.reload.status
66	    assert_raises(Docker::Error::NotFoundError) do
67	      Docker::Container.get(container_id)
68	    end
69	  end
70	
71	  test "create handles Docker errors gracefully" do
72	    DockerMock.inject_failure(:create)
73	
74	    assert_raises(Docker::Error::DockerError) do

76	      @manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: @sandbox, user: @user)
77	    end
78	  end
79	
80	  test "ensure_image pulls image if not present" do
81	    image_name = "ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest"
82	
83	    # Image should not exist initially
84	    assert_raises(Docker::Error::NotFoundError) do

95	  test "create_snapshot creates DB record and Docker image" do
96	    @sandbox.update!(container_id: nil, status: "pending")
97	    @manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: @sandbox, user: @user)
98	    @sandbox.reload

100	    snap = @manager.create_snapshot(sandbox: @sandbox, name: "test-snap")
101	
102	    assert_kind_of Snapshot, snap
103	    assert_equal "test-snap", snap.name
104	    assert_equal @sandbox.name, snap.source_sandbox
105	    assert snap.docker_image.present?

107	
108	    # Verify Docker image was committed

112	  test "create_snapshot with label stores label" do
113	    @sandbox.update!(container_id: nil, status: "pending")
114	    @manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: @sandbox, user: @user)
115	    @sandbox.reload

117	    snap = @manager.create_snapshot(sandbox: @sandbox, name: "labeled-snap", label: "before migration")
118	    assert_equal "before migration", snap.label

133	    # Create a snapshot record
134	    Snapshot.create!(user: @user, name: "listed-snap", docker_image: "sc-snap-alice:listed-snap", docker_size: 100)
135	
136	    snapshots = @manager.list_snapshots(user: @user)
137	    assert snapshots.any? { |s| s[:name] == "listed-snap" }

142	    @manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: @sandbox, user: @user)
143	    @sandbox.reload

145	    snap = @manager.create_snapshot(sandbox: @sandbox, name: "to-destroy")
146	    image_ref = snap.docker_image

149	
150	    assert_nil Snapshot.find_by(user: @user, name: "to-destroy")
151	    assert_raises(Docker::Error::NotFoundError) do

157	    assert_raises(SandboxManager::Error) do
158	      @manager.destroy_snapshot(user: @user, name: "nonexistent-snap")
159	    end

162	  test "legacy snapshot method returns hash" do
163	    @sandbox.update!(container_id: nil, status: "pending")
164	    @manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: @sandbox, user: @user)
165	    @sandbox.reload

168

173	
174	  # Regression test: bind-mounted dirs must be reset to 777 on restart.
175	  # After a Sysbox container run, dirs are re-owned by the Sysbox-remapped UID
176	  # (e.g. 166537) so the Rails process can't chmod them without sudo.
177	  test "start calls ensure_mount_dirs for sandboxes with mount_home to reset bind-mount permissions" do
178	    @sandbox.update!(mount_home: true, status: "stopped", container_id: nil)
179	
180	    ensure_called = false
181	    manager = Class.new(SandboxManager) {
182	      define_method(:ensure_mount_dirs) { |_user, _sandbox| ensure_called = true }
183	    }.new
184	    manager.start(sandbox: @sandbox)
185	
186	    assert ensure_called,
187	      "SandboxManager#start must call ensure_mount_dirs before creating the container " \
188	      "so the home dir is reset to 777; without this, Sysbox UID remapping leaves the " \
189	      "dir owned by nobody (chmod 755) and breaks SSH StrictModes and VNC ~/.Xauthority"
190	  end
191	
192	  test "start calls ensure_mount_dirs for sandboxes with data_path to reset bind-mount permissions" do
193	    @sandbox.update!(data_path: "mydata", status: "stopped", container_id: nil)
194	
195	    ensure_called = false
196	    manager = Class.new(SandboxManager) {
197	      define_method(:ensure_mount_dirs) { |_user, _sandbox| ensure_called = true }
198	    }.new
199	    manager.start(sandbox: @sandbox)
200	
201	    assert ensure_called,
202	      "SandboxManager#start must call ensure_mount_dirs for data_path sandboxes too"
203	  end
204	end

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/sandbox_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "    # Chrome profile persistence: mount separately if not mounting full home\n    if user.chrome_persist_profile? && !sandbox.mount_home\n      host_path = \"#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/chrome-profile\"\n      binds << \"#{host_path}:/home/#{user.name}/.config/google-chrome\"\n    end\n    binds\n  end\nend",
  "new_string": "    # Chrome profile persistence: mount separately if not mounting full home\n    if user.chrome_persist_profile? && !sandbox.mount_home\n      host_path = \"#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/chrome-profile\"\n      binds << \"#{host_path}:/home/#{user.name}/.config/google-chrome\"\n    end\n    # Per-user persisted dotdirs (Claude/Codex auth, etc.) — only when full home isn't mounted\n    if !sandbox.mount_home\n      user.persisted_paths.find_each do |pp|\n        host_path = \"#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/persisted/#{pp.path}\"\n        binds << \"#{host_path}:/home/#{user.name}/#{pp.path}\"\n      end\n    end\n    binds\n  end\n\n  # Drop per-user files into a running container via `docker exec` so secrets\n  # never appear in container env or `docker inspect`. Mirrors set_smb_password.\n  def inject_files(container, user)\n    user.injected_files.find_each do |f|\n      content = f.content.to_s\n      script = <<~SH\n        set -e\n        target=\"/home/$1/$2\"\n        mkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$target\")\"\n        printf '%s' \"$3\" > \"$target\"\n        chmod \"$4\" \"$target\"\n        chown \"$1:$1\" \"$target\" 2>/dev/null || true\n      SH\n      container.exec([ \"bash\", \"-c\", script, \"_\", user.name, f.path, content, format(\"%o\", f.mode) ])\n    rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e\n      Rails.logger.warn(\"inject_files: failed to write #{f.path} for #{user.name}: #{e.message}\")\n    end\n  end\nend"
}

  "old_string": "  def update_ssh_keys\n    @user = Current.user\n    keys = (params[:ssh_keys] || []).reject { |k| k[:key].blank? }.map do |k|\n      { \"name\" => k[:name].to_s.strip.presence || \"key-#{SecureRandom.hex(3)}\", \"key\" => k[:key].to_s.strip }\n    end\n\n    if @user.update(ssh_keys: keys)\n      redirect_to settings_path, notice: \"SSH keys updated.\"\n    else\n      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(\", \")\n    end\n  end",
  "new_string": "  def update_ssh_keys\n    @user = Current.user\n    keys = (params[:ssh_keys] || []).reject { |k| k[:key].blank? }.map do |k|\n      { \"name\" => k[:name].to_s.strip.presence || \"key-#{SecureRandom.hex(3)}\", \"key\" => k[:key].to_s.strip }\n    end\n\n    if @user.update(ssh_keys: keys)\n      redirect_to settings_path, notice: \"SSH keys updated.\"\n    else\n      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(\", \")\n    end\n  end\n\n  def update_persisted_paths\n    @user = Current.user\n    paths = (params[:persisted_paths] || []).map { |p| p[:path].to_s.strip.chomp(\"/\") }.reject(&:blank?).uniq\n\n    PersistedPath.transaction do\n      @user.persisted_paths.where.not(path: paths).destroy_all\n      paths.each { |p| @user.persisted_paths.find_or_create_by(path: p) }\n    end\n\n    redirect_to settings_path, notice: \"Persisted directories updated.\"\n  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordInvalid => e\n    redirect_to settings_path, alert: e.message\n  end\n\n  def update_injected_files\n    @user = Current.user\n    rows = (params[:injected_files] || []).reject { |r| r[:path].blank? }\n\n    InjectedFile.transaction do\n      kept_paths = rows.map { |r| r[:path].to_s.strip }\n      @user.injected_files.where.not(path: kept_paths).destroy_all\n\n      rows.each do |row|\n        path = row[:path].to_s.strip\n        record = @user.injected_files.find_or_initialize_by(path: path)\n        # Empty content on existing record = leave as-is (lets users edit path/mode without re-uploading content)\n        record.content = row[:content] if row[:content].present? || record.new_record?\n        record.mode = row[:mode].to_i if row[:mode].present?\n        record.save!\n      end\n    end\n\n    redirect_to settings_path, notice: \"Injected files updated.\"\n  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordInvalid => e\n    redirect_to settings_path, alert: e.message\n  end\n\n  def delete_injected_file\n    @user = Current.user\n    @user.injected_files.find(params[:id]).destroy!\n    redirect_to settings_path, notice: \"Injected file removed.\"\n  end"
}

1	class SandboxesController < ApplicationController
2	  before_action :set_sandbox, only: [ :show, :update, :destroy, :start, :stop, :rebuild, :retry, :logs, :metrics ]
3	  before_action :set_archived_sandbox, only: [ :archive_restore, :purge ]
4	
5	  def new
6	    authorize Sandbox
7	    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
8	    @btrfs_available = BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
9	    @defaults = Setting.instance
10	  end
11	
12	  def show
13	    @sandbox_snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
14	                           .select { |s| s[:source_sandbox] == @sandbox.name }
15	    @routes = @sandbox.routes.order(:created_at)
16	    @btrfs = BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
17	  end
18	
19	  def update
20	    if @sandbox.update(params.require(:sandbox).permit(:name))
21	      redirect_to @sandbox, notice: "Sandbox renamed to #{@sandbox.name}."
22	    else
23	      @sandbox_snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
24	                             .select { |s| s[:source_sandbox] == @sandbox.name }
25	      @routes = @sandbox.routes.order(:created_at)
26	      @btrfs = BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
27	      flash.now[:alert] = @sandbox.errors.full_messages.join(", ")
28	      render :show, status: :unprocessable_entity
29	    end
30	  end
31	
32	  def create
33	    authorize Sandbox
34	
35	    from_snapshot_name = params[:snapshot].presence
36	
37	    image = if from_snapshot_name.present?
38	      snap = Snapshot.find_by(user: Current.user, name: from_snapshot_name)
39	      snap&.docker_image || "sc-snap-#{Current.user.name}:#{from_snapshot_name}"
40	    else
41	      params[:image].presence || SandboxManager::DEFAULT_IMAGE
42	    end
43	
44	    # Build sandbox record
45	    # Note: temporary sandboxes can only be created via CLI
46	    defaults = Setting.instance
47	    sandbox = Current.user.sandboxes.build(
48	      name: params.require(:name),
49	      status: "pending",
50	      image: image,
51	      mount_home: params[:mount_home] == "1",
52	      data_path: params[:mount_data] == "1" ? (params[:data_path].presence || ".") : nil,
53	      tailscale: params[:tailscale] == "1",
54	      vnc_enabled: params[:vnc_enabled] != "0",
55	      vnc_geometry: Sandbox::VNC_GEOMETRIES.include?(params[:vnc_geometry]) ? params[:vnc_geometry] : "1280x900",
56	      vnc_depth: Sandbox::VNC_DEPTHS.include?(params[:vnc_depth].to_i) ? params[:vnc_depth].to_i : 24,
57	      docker_enabled: params[:docker_enabled] != "0",
58	      smb_enabled: params[:smb_enabled] == "1",
59	      temporary: false
60	    )
61	
62	    if sandbox.save
63	      # Enqueue async job
64	      SandboxProvisionJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: sandbox.id)
65	
66	      # Optimistic redirect - dashboard will update via Turbo
67	      redirect_to root_path, notice: "Creating sandcastle #{sandbox.name}..."
68	    else
69	      @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
70	      flash.now[:alert] = "Failed to create sandbox: #{sandbox.errors.full_messages.join(', ')}"
71	      render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
72	    end
73	  rescue => e
74	    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
75	    flash.now[:alert] = "Failed to create sandbox: #{e.message}"
76	    render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
77	  end
78	
79	  def metrics
80	    points = @sandbox.container_metrics.recent
81	      .map { |m| { t: m.recorded_at.to_i, cpu: m.cpu_percent, mem: m.memory_mb.round(0) } }
82	
83	    # Append current live stats so graphs render instantly on page load
84	    if @sandbox.status == "running" && @sandbox.container_id.present?
85	      full = Rails.cache.fetch("sandbox_stats_full:#{@sandbox.id}", expires_in: 4.seconds) do
86	        raw = Docker::Container.get(@sandbox.container_id).stats(stream: false)
87	        next nil if raw.blank?
88	        {
89	          cpu_percent: StatsCalculator.cpu_percent(raw),
90	          memory_mb: StatsCalculator.memory_mb(raw),
91	          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "limit") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
92	          net_rx: 0, net_tx: 0, disk_read: 0, disk_write: 0, pids: 0
93	        }
94	      rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
95	        nil
96	      end
97	      points << { t: Time.current.to_i, cpu: full[:cpu_percent], mem: full[:memory_mb].round(0) } if full
98	    end
99	
100	    render json: points
101	  end
102	
103	  def logs
104	    tail = (params[:tail] || 200).to_i.clamp(1, 5000)
105	    @tail = tail
106	    @logs = SandboxManager.new.logs(sandbox: @sandbox, tail: tail, timestamps: true)
107	  rescue SandboxManager::Error => e
108	    @logs = nil
109	    @log_error = e.message
110	  end
111	
112	  def destroy
113	    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
114	      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
115	      return
116	    end
117	
118	    user = Current.user
119	    archive = user.effective_archive_retention_days > 0
120	
121	    @sandbox.start_job("destroying")
122	    SandboxDestroyJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id, archive: archive)
123	
124	    notice = archive ? "Archiving sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." : "Destroying sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..."
125	    respond_to do |format|
126	      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: notice }
127	      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
128	    end
129	  end
130	
131	  def archive_restore
132	    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
133	      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
134	      return
135	    end
136	
137	    @sandbox.start_job("restoring")
138	    SandboxRestoreJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
139	    redirect_to root_path, notice: "Restoring sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..."
140	  end
141	
142	  def purge
143	    authorize @sandbox, :purge?
144	    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
145	      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
146	      return
147	    end
148	
149	    @sandbox.start_job("destroying")
150	    SandboxDestroyJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id, archive: false)
151	    redirect_to root_path, notice: "Permanently deleting sandcastle..."
152	  end
153	
154	  def start
155	    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
156	      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
157	      return
158	    end
159	
160	    @sandbox.start_job("starting")
161	    SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
162	
163	    respond_to do |format|
164	      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Starting sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
165	      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
166	    end
167	  end
168	
169	  def stop
170	    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
171	      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
172	      return
173	    end
174	
175	    @sandbox.start_job("stopping")
176	    SandboxStopJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
177	
178	    respond_to do |format|
179	      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Stopping sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
180	      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
181	    end
182	  end
183	
184	  def rebuild
185	    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
186	      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
187	      return
188	    end
189	
190	    @sandbox.start_job("rebuilding")
191	    SandboxRebuildJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
192	
193	    respond_to do |format|
194	      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Rebuilding sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
195	      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
196	    end
197	  end
198	
199	  def retry
200	    return unless @sandbox.job_failed?
201	
202	    @sandbox.update!(job_error: nil)
203	
204	    case @sandbox.status
205	    when "destroyed", "pending"
206	      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Cannot retry creation. Please create a new sandbox."
207	    when "stopped"
208	      SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
209	      redirect_to root_path, notice: "Retrying start..."
210	    when "running"
211	      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Sandbox is already running"
212	    end
213	  end
214	
215	  private
216	
217	  def set_sandbox
218	    @sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
219	    authorize @sandbox
220	  end
221	
222	  def set_archived_sandbox
223	    @sandbox = Current.user.sandboxes.archived.find(params[:id])
224	    authorize @sandbox, :archive_restore?
225	  end
226	end

  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/home_file_discovery.rb",
  "content": "class HomeFileDiscovery\n  class Error < StandardError; end\n\n  PREFIX_IGNORE = %w[\n    .cache/\n    .npm/_cacache/\n    .npm/_logs/\n    .local/share/Trash/\n    .local/share/mise/\n    .vscode-server/\n    .config/configstore/\n    node_modules/\n  ].freeze\n\n  EXACT_IGNORE = %w[\n    .bash_history\n    .zsh_history\n    .lesshst\n    .python_history\n    .node_repl_history\n    .Xauthority\n    .ssh/known_hosts\n    .gnupg/random_seed\n    .DS_Store\n    .viminfo\n  ].freeze\n\n  SUGGESTIONS = [\n    [ %r{\\A\\.claude/},          \"bind\",   \"Claude rewrites credentials on token refresh\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.codex/},           \"bind\",   \"Codex rewrites credentials on token refresh\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.config/gh/},       \"bind\",   \"gh rewrites tokens on refresh\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.aws/sso/},         \"bind\",   \"AWS SSO rewrites tokens hourly\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.config/gcloud/},   \"bind\",   \"gcloud rewrites tokens\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.kube/},            \"bind\",   \"kubectl exec plugins rewrite tokens\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.gitconfig\\z},      \"inject\", \"static config\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.npmrc\\z},          \"inject\", \"static token\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.aws/credentials\\z}, \"inject\", \"static credentials\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.vimrc\\z},          \"inject\", \"dotfile\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.tmux\\.conf\\z},     \"inject\", \"dotfile\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.zshrc\\z},          \"inject\", \"dotfile\" ],\n    [ %r{\\A\\.bashrc\\z},         \"inject\", \"dotfile\" ]\n  ].freeze\n\n  def initialize(sandbox)\n    @sandbox = sandbox\n    @user = sandbox.user\n  end\n\n  def call\n    return [] if @sandbox.container_id.blank?\n\n    container = Docker::Container.get(@sandbox.container_id)\n    raw = run_diff(container)\n    paths = raw.lines.map(&:chomp).reject(&:blank?)\n    ignored = @user.ignored_paths.pluck(:path).to_set\n\n    paths\n      .reject { |p| ignored?(p, ignored) }\n      .map { |p| classify(p) }\n  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n    []\n  end\n\n  def fetch_content(path)\n    return nil if @sandbox.container_id.blank?\n    container = Docker::Container.get(@sandbox.container_id)\n    out = container.exec([\n      \"bash\", \"-c\",\n      \"cat \\\"/home/$1/$2\\\" 2>/dev/null\",\n      \"_\", @user.name, path\n    ])\n    extract_stdout(out)\n  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n    nil\n  end\n\n  private\n\n  def run_diff(container)\n    script = <<~SH\n      baseline=\"#{SandboxManager::HOME_BASELINE_PATH}\"\n      if [ ! -f \"$baseline\" ]; then exit 0; fi\n      find /home/\"$1\" -xdev -type f -printf '%P\\\\n' 2>/dev/null | sort | comm -23 - \"$baseline\"\n    SH\n    out = container.exec([ \"bash\", \"-c\", script, \"_\", @user.name ])\n    extract_stdout(out)\n  end\n\n  def extract_stdout(out)\n    case out\n    when Array\n      first = out[0]\n      first.is_a?(Array) ? first.join : first.to_s\n    else\n      out.to_s\n    end\n  end\n\n  def ignored?(path, ignored_set)\n    return true if ignored_set.include?(path)\n    return true if EXACT_IGNORE.include?(path)\n    PREFIX_IGNORE.any? { |pre| path.start_with?(pre) }\n  end\n\n  def classify(path)\n    SUGGESTIONS.each do |regex, action, reason|\n      return { path: path, suggested: action, reason: reason } if path.match?(regex)\n    end\n    { path: path, suggested: \"ignore\", reason: \"no rule matched — pick the right action manually\" }\n  end\nend\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/sandboxes_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "  def retry\n    return unless @sandbox.job_failed?",
  "new_string": "  def discover_files\n    @results = HomeFileDiscovery.new(@sandbox).call\n  end\n\n  def promote_file\n    path = params[:path].to_s\n    action = params[:action_type].to_s\n    user = Current.user\n\n    if path.blank?\n      redirect_back fallback_location: sandbox_path(@sandbox), alert: \"Missing path.\"\n      return\n    end\n\n    case action\n    when \"bind\"\n      bind_path = File.dirname(path)\n      bind_path = path if bind_path == \".\"\n      user.persisted_paths.find_or_create_by!(path: bind_path)\n      notice = \"Persisting #{bind_path}/ on next sandbox start.\"\n    when \"inject\"\n      content = HomeFileDiscovery.new(@sandbox).fetch_content(path)\n      record = user.injected_files.find_or_initialize_by(path: path)\n      record.content = content\n      record.save!\n      notice = \"Will inject #{path} on next sandbox start.\"\n    when \"ignore\"\n      user.ignored_paths.find_or_create_by!(path: path)\n      notice = \"Ignoring #{path}.\"\n    else\n      redirect_back fallback_location: sandbox_path(@sandbox), alert: \"Unknown action.\"\n      return\n    end\n\n    redirect_to sandbox_path(@sandbox, anchor: \"discover\"), notice: notice\n  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordInvalid => e\n    redirect_back fallback_location: sandbox_path(@sandbox), alert: e.message\n  end\n\n  def retry\n    return unless @sandbox.job_failed?"
}

1	class SandboxPolicy < ApplicationPolicy
2	  def index?                 = true
3	  def show?                  = owner_only?
4	  def create?                = true
5	  def update?                = owner_only?
6	  def destroy?               = owner_or_admin?
7	  def start?                 = owner_or_admin?
8	  def stop?                  = owner_or_admin?
9	  def rebuild?               = owner_or_admin?
10	  def retry?                 = owner_only?
11	  def logs?                  = owner_or_admin?
12	  def stats?                 = owner_or_admin?
13	  def metrics?               = owner_or_admin?
14	  def card?                  = owner_only?
15	  def connect?               = owner_only?
16	  def snapshot?              = owner_only?
17	  def restore?               = owner_only?
18	  def archive_restore?       = owner_or_admin?
19	  def purge?                 = owner_or_admin?
20	  def tailscale_connect?     = owner_only?
21	  def tailscale_disconnect?  = owner_only?
22	
23	  private
24	
25	  def owner_only?
26	    record.user_id == user.id
27	  end
28	
29	  def owner_or_admin?

{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/test/models/injected_file_test.rb",
  "content": "require \"test_helper\"\n\nclass InjectedFileTest < ActiveSupport::TestCase\n  setup { @user = users(:one) }\n\n  test \"valid record\" do\n    f = @user.injected_files.build(path: \".npmrc\", content: \"//registry.npmjs.org/:_authToken=secret\")\n    assert f.valid?, f.errors.full_messages.inspect\n  end\n\n  test \"default mode is 0600\" do\n    f = @user.injected_files.create!(path: \".npmrc\", content: \"x\")\n    assert_equal 0o600, f.mode\n  end\n\n  test \"rejects absolute path\" do\n    f = @user.injected_files.build(path: \"/etc/passwd\", content: \"x\")\n    assert_not f.valid?\n    assert_includes f.errors[:path].to_s, \"relative\"\n  end\n\n  test \"rejects path with .. segment\" do\n    f = @user.injected_files.build(path: \"../etc/shadow\", content: \"x\")\n    assert_not f.valid?\n  end\n\n  test \"rejects path with . segment\" do\n    f = @user.injected_files.build(path: \"./foo\", content: \"x\")\n    assert_not f.valid?\n  end\n\n  test \"rejects empty path segments\" do\n    f = @user.injected_files.build(path: \"foo//bar\", content: \"x\")\n    assert_not f.valid?\n  end\n\n  test \"uniqueness scoped to user\" do\n    @user.injected_files.create!(path: \".same\", content: \"a\")\n    dup = @user.injected_files.build(path: \".same\", content: \"b\")\n    assert_not dup.valid?\n    assert_includes dup.errors[:path].to_s, \"taken\"\n  end\n\n  test \"different users can have same path\" do\n    @user.injected_files.create!(path: \".shared\", content: \"a\")\n    other = users(:two).injected_files.build(path: \".shared\", content: \"b\")\n    assert other.valid?, other.errors.full_messages.inspect\n  end\n\n  test \"content is encrypted at rest\" do\n    plain = \"ANTHROPIC_OAUTH_REFRESH_TOKEN=very-secret\"\n    rec = @user.injected_files.create!(path: \".secrets\", content: plain)\n    raw = ActiveRecord::Base.connection.select_value(\"SELECT content FROM injected_files WHERE id = #{rec.id}\")\n    assert_not_includes raw.to_s, \"very-secret\"\n    assert_equal plain, rec.reload.content\n  end\n\n  test \"mode out of range is rejected\" do\n    f = @user.injected_files.build(path: \".x\", content: \"x\", mode: 0o7777)\n    assert_not f.valid?\n  end\nend\n"
}

  "content": "require \"test_helper\"\n\nclass PersistedPathTest < ActiveSupport::TestCase\n  setup { @user = users(:one) }\n\n  test \"valid record\" do\n    p = @user.persisted_paths.build(path: \".my-tool\")\n    assert p.valid?, p.errors.full_messages.inspect\n  end\n\n  test \"rejects absolute path\" do\n    p = @user.persisted_paths.build(path: \"/etc/foo\")\n    assert_not p.valid?\n  end\n\n  test \"rejects path traversal\" do\n    assert_not @user.persisted_paths.build(path: \"../escape\").valid?\n    assert_not @user.persisted_paths.build(path: \"./here\").valid?\n    assert_not @user.persisted_paths.build(path: \"a//b\").valid?\n  end\n\n  test \"normalizes trailing slash\" do\n    p = @user.persisted_paths.create!(path: \".claude/\")\n    assert_equal \".claude\", p.path\n  end\n\n  test \"normalizes whitespace\" do\n    p = @user.persisted_paths.create!(path: \"  .codex  \")\n    assert_equal \".codex\", p.path\n  end\n\n  test \"uniqueness scoped to user\" do\n    @user.persisted_paths.find_or_create_by!(path: \".unique-test\")\n    dup = @user.persisted_paths.build(path: \".unique-test\")\n    assert_not dup.valid?\n  end\nend\n"
}

   # Pre-create container record in the mock so Docker::Container.get(...) works\n    DockerMock.containers[@sandbox.container_id] = {\n      \"Id\" => @sandbox.container_id,\n      \"State\" => { \"Status\" => \"running\", \"Running\" => true }\n    }\n  end\n\n  test \"returns empty when sandbox has no container\" do\n    @sandbox.update!(container_id: nil)\n    assert_equal [], HomeFileDiscovery.new(@sandbox).call\n  end\n\n  test \"filters out exact-ignore paths\" do\n    DockerMock.exec_response = [ [ \".bash_history\\n.something-real\\n\" ], [], 0 ]\n    results = HomeFileDiscovery.new(@sandbox).call\n    paths = results.map { |r| r[:path] }\n    assert_includes paths, \".something-real\"\n    assert_not_includes paths, \".bash_history\"\n  end\n\n  test \"filters out prefix-ignore paths\" do\n    DockerMock.exec_response = [ [ \".cache/foo/bar\\n.local/share/Trash/x\\n.real-file\\n\" ], [], 0 ]\n    paths = HomeFileDiscovery.new(@sandbox).call.map { |r| r[:path] }\n    assert_equal [ \".real-file\" ], paths\n  end\n\n  test \"filters per-user IgnoredPath entries\" do\n    @user.ignored_paths.create!(path: \".user-ignored\")\n    DockerMock.exec_response = [ [ \".user-ignored\\n.kept\\n\" ], [], 0 ]\n    paths = HomeFileDiscovery.new(@sandbox).call.map { |r| r[:path] }\n    assert_equal [ \".kept\" ], paths\n  end\n\n  test \"suggests bind for known OAuth dirs\" do\n    DockerMock.exec_response = [ [ \".claude/.credentials.json\\n\" ], [], 0 ]\n    row = HomeFileDiscovery.new(@sandbox).call.first\n    assert_equal \"bind\", row[:suggested]\n  end\n\n  test \"suggests inject for known static config files\" do\n    DockerMock.exec_response = [ [ \".npmrc\\n\" ], [], 0 ]\n    row = HomeFileDiscovery.new(@sandbox).call.first\n    assert_equal \"inject\", row[:suggested]\n  end\n\n  test \"suggests ignore for unrecognized files\" do\n    DockerMock.exec_response = [ [ \".some-novel-file\\n\" ], [], 0 ]\n    row = HomeFileDiscovery.new(@sandbox).call.first\n    assert_equal \"ignore\", row[:suggested]\n  end\n\n  test \"fetch_content runs cat in container\" do\n    DockerMock.exec_response = ->(_cmd) { [ [ \"file body here\" ], [], 0 ] }\n    content = HomeFileDiscovery.new(@sandbox).fetch_content(\".claude/.credentials.json\")\n    assert_equal \"file body here\", content\n    last = DockerMock.exec_calls.last\n    assert_match(/\\Acat /, last[:cmd][2])\n  end\n\n  test \"discovery exec uses the baseline path constant\" do\n    DockerMock.exec_response = [ [ \"\" ], [], 0 ]\n    HomeFileDiscovery.new(@sandbox).call\n    last = DockerMock.exec_calls.last\n    assert_includes last[:cmd][2], SandboxManager::HOME_BASELINE_PATH\n  end\nend\n"
}

 end\n\n  test \"volume_binds skips persisted paths when full home is mounted\" do\n    @user.persisted_paths.find_or_create_by!(path: \".claude\")\n    @sandbox.update!(mount_home: true)\n\n    binds ```
module SessionTestHelper
  def sign_in_as(user)
    Current.session = user.sessions.create!

    ActionDispatch::TestRequest.create.cookie_jar.tap do |cookie_jar|
      cookie_jar.signed[:session_id] = Current.session.id
      cookies["session_id"] = cookie_jar[:session_id]
    end
  end

  def sign_out
    Current.session&.destroy!
    cookies.delete("session_id")
  end
end

ActiveSupport.on_load(:action_dispatch_integration_test) do
  include SessionTestHelper
end
```

  # Drop per-user files into a running container via `docker exec` so secrets
  # never appear in container env or `docker inspect`. Mirrors set_smb_password.
  def inject_files(container, user)
    user.injected_files.find_each do |f|
      content = f.content.to_s
      script = <<~SH
        set -e
        target="/home/$1/$2"
        mkdir -p "$(dirname "$target")"
        printf '%s' "$3" > "$target"
        chmod "$4" "$target"
        chown "$1:$1" "$target" 2>/dev/null || true
      SH
      container.exec([ "bash", "-c", script, "_", user.name, f.path, content, format("%o", f.mode) ])
    rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
      Rails.logger.warn("inject_files: failed to write #{f.path} for #{user.name}: #{e.message}")
    end
  end
end</tool_use_error>
```

  "new_string": "  def update_injected_files\n    @user = Current.user\n    rows = (params[:injected_files] || []).reject { |r| r[:path].blank? }\n\n    InjectedFile.transaction do\n      kept_paths = rows.map { |r| r[:path].to_s.strip }\n      @user.injected_files.where.not(path: kept_paths).destroy_all\n\n      rows.each do |row|\n        path = row[:path].to_s.strip\n        record = @user.injected_files.find_or_initialize_by(path: path)\n        # Empty content on existing record = leave as-is (lets users edit path/mode without re-uploading content)\n        record.content = row[:content] if row[:content].present? || record.new_record?\n        # Mode is always octal in form input (\"600\" → 0o600 = 384 decimal)\n        record.mode = row[:mode].to_s.to_i(8) if row[:mode].present?\n        record.save!\n      end\n    end\n\n    redirect_to settings_path, notice: \"Injected files updated.\"\n  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordInvalid => e\n    redirect_to settings_path, alert: e.message\n  end"

> AGENT

Done. **191 tests pass, 0 failures.** New code is rubocop-clean (the 2 lint hits are in pre-existing `set_smb_password`, not mine).

## What got built

| Layer | Files |
|---|---|
| Migrations | `db/migrate/20260416100001_create_injected_files.rb`, `..._create_persisted_paths.rb` (with .claude/.codex backfill for existing users), `..._create_ignored_paths.rb` |
| Models | `app/models/injected_file.rb` (encrypts content, validates path), `persisted_path.rb` (normalizes, validates), `ignored_path.rb`. `user.rb` gets the 3 associations + `after_create_commit :seed_default_persisted_paths` |
| Service | `app/services/sandbox_manager.rb` extended: public `inject_files` (post-start `docker exec`, mirrors `set_smb_password`), public `write_home_baseline`, dynamic `volume_binds`/`ensure_mount_dirs` for persisted paths. New `app/services/home_file_discovery.rb` with prefix/exact ignore lists + suggestion rules |
| Controllers | `settings_controller.rb`: `update_persisted_paths`, `update_injected_files`, `delete_injected_file`. `sandboxes_controller.rb`: `discover_files` (GET) + `promote_file` (POST) |
| Routes & policy | `routes.rb` 5 new routes; `sandbox_policy.rb` adds `discover_files?`/`promote_file?` |
| UI | `settings/show.html.erb` gains "Persisted Directories" + "Injected Files" sections (mode is octal — "600" → 0o600). `sandboxes/show.html.erb` gets a "Discover Files" link. New `sandboxes/discover_files.html.erb` table with Bind/Inject/Ignore action buttons. Stimulus: `persisted_paths_controller.js`, `injected_files_controller.js` |
| Image | `images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh` creates `/var/sandcastle/` so the baseline file has a home |
| Tests (51 new) | 19 model + 16 service + 16 controller across `test/models/{injected_file,persisted_path,ignored_path,user_persistence_seeding}_test.rb`, `test/services/{home_file_discovery,sandbox_manager_inject_files}_test.rb`, `test/controllers/{settings,sandboxes_controller_discovery}_test.rb`. DockerMock extended with `Container#exec` capture + configurable response |

## Live integration check

```
User: thies → persisted_paths: [".claude", ".codex"]   (seeded by migration)
Volume binds for thies's sandbox:
  /tmp/.../users/thies/persisted/.claude → /home/thies/.claude
  /tmp/.../users/thies/persisted/.codex  → /home/thies/.codex
```

Migration auto-backfilled `.claude` and `.codex` for the existing `thies` user; new user signups get them via `after_create_commit`.

## What "e2e" did and didn't cover

**Covered** (rails test, 191 runs):
- Full request → controller → service → DB cycle for every new endpoint
- DockerMock simulates `container.exec` so `inject_files`, `write_home_baseline`, and discovery's `find | comm` all execute the real production code paths and have their args asserted
- Encryption-at-rest verified by reading raw column bytes after writing plaintext

**Not covered** (would need a real Docker host):
- A real `docker exec` actually creating the file inside a sandbox
- The `comm -23` baseline diff against a real `find /home/$USER` output
- Browser-driven click-through of the Settings UI / Discover button

The dev compose stack (`sandcastle-pr81-postgres-1` etc.) is up but on the `pr81` branch's web image — running these flows live would mean rebuilding the web container off this branch (`mise run deploy:dev`) and exercising it from a browser. Happy to do that if you want — just say the word and I'll bring it up and walk through the discovery flow with you.

## One pre-existing test issue worth flagging

`test/services/sandbox_manager_test.rb` (the *original* tests, not mine) actually fail with `Errno::EROFS: /data` unless you set `SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR`. I worked around this with `SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR=/tmp/sandcastle-test-data` for the run. Worth either adding a default in `test_helper.rb` or making the directory creation idempotent in `prepare_traefik_config`. Not in scope for this task; flagging it because it'll bite future test runs.

diff --git a/images/sandbox/Dockerfile b/images/sandbox/Dockerfile
index e19b076..dcf513f 100644
--- a/images/sandbox/Dockerfile
+++ b/images/sandbox/Dockerfile
@@ -8,9 +8,25 @@ ARG SANDCASTLE_VERSION=dev
 
 # Always pull latest security patches and package updates.
 # CACHE_DATE is set at build time to bust the layer cache on demand.
+#
+# Hold Docker-stack packages before upgrading: the base image pins
+# containerd.io=1.7.27-1 (see Dockerfile.base for rationale — containerd.io ≥ 2.x
+# breaks sysbox-runc, nestybox/sysbox#958). Without the hold, `apt-get upgrade`
+# installs containerd.io 2.x, which also replaces /usr/bin/runc with a newer
+# runc (1.3.3+) whose CVE-2025-52881 fix rejects sysbox's FUSE-backed procfs
+# with "unsafe procfs detected" and breaks docker-in-docker inside sandboxes.
+# Re-install the pinned runc afterwards as belt-and-suspenders in case an
+# older base image (built before the apt-mark hold was added) is used.
 ARG CACHE_DATE=0
-RUN for i in 1 2 3; do apt-get update && break || sleep 5; done \
-    && apt-get upgrade -y && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
+RUN apt-mark hold docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin \
+    && for i in 1 2 3; do apt-get update && break || sleep 5; done \
+    && apt-get upgrade -y \
+    && RUNC_VERSION="v1.1.15" \
+    && ARCH=$(dpkg --print-architecture) \
+    && curl -fsSL "https://github.com/opencontainers/runc/releases/download/${RUNC_VERSION}/runc.${ARCH}" \
+       -o /usr/bin/runc \
+    && chmod +x /usr/bin/runc \
+    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
 
 # Bake version into the image
 RUN echo "${SANDCASTLE_VERSION}" > /etc/sandcastle-version
diff --git a/images/sandbox/Dockerfile.base b/images/sandbox/Dockerfile.base
index 72947f1..cda8d65 100644
--- a/images/sandbox/Dockerfile.base
+++ b/images/sandbox/Dockerfile.base
@@ -84,6 +84,7 @@ RUN install -m 0755 -d /etc/apt/keyrings \
          containerd.io=1.7.27-1 \
          docker-buildx-plugin=0.24.0-1~ubuntu.24.04~noble \
          docker-compose-plugin=2.40.3-1~ubuntu.24.04~noble \
+    && apt-mark hold docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin \
     && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
 
 # Pin runc — multiple runc versions break inside sysbox containers:
